```unrealscript
function void TakeDamage(Damage: int, instigatedBy: Pawn, HitLocation: Vector, Momentum: Vector, DamageType: name)
{
    if (Role >= 4) goto L0044;
    Log(self $ " client damage type " $ DamageType $ " by " $ instigatedBy);
    return;
    L0044:
    bAlreadyDead = Health <= 0;
    if (Physics != 0) goto L0067;
    SetMovementPhysics();
    L0067:
    if (Physics != 1) goto L009A;
    Momentum.Z = FMax(Momentum.Z, 0.4 * VSize(Momentum));
    L009A:
    if (instigatedBy != self) goto L00B1;
    Momentum *= 0.6;
    L00B1:
    Momentum = Momentum / Mass;
    AddVelocity(Momentum);
    actualDamage = Level.Game.ReduceDamage(Damage, DamageType, self, instigatedBy);
    if (bIsPlayer) goto L015E;
    if (ReducedDamageType != 'All') goto L011E;
    actualDamage = 0;
    goto L015B;
    L011E:
    if (Inventory == None) goto L0150;
    actualDamage = Inventory.ReduceDamage(actualDamage, DamageType, HitLocation);
    goto L015B;
    L0150:
    actualDamage = Damage;
    L015B:
    goto L021A;
    L015E:
    if (!(instigatedBy != None && (instigatedBy.IsA(Class.Name) || self.IsA(instigatedBy.Class.Name)))) goto L01D1;
    actualDamage = actualDamage * FMin(1.0 - ReducedDamagePct, 0.35);
    goto L021A;
    L01D1:
    if (!(ReducedDamageType == 'All' || ReducedDamageType != 'None' && ReducedDamageType == DamageType)) goto L021A;
    actualDamage = actualDamage * (1 - ReducedDamagePct);
    L021A:
    if (Level.Game.DamageMutator == None) goto L0272;
    Level.Game.DamageMutator.MutatorTakeDamage(actualDamage, self, instigatedBy, HitLocation, Momentum, DamageType);
    L0272:
    Health -= actualDamage;
    if (carriedDecoration == None) goto L028F;
    DropDecoration();
    L028F:
    if (HitLocation != vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)) goto L02B1;
    HitLocation = Location;
    L02B1:
    if (Health <= 0) goto L02FD;
    if (!(instigatedBy != None && instigatedBy != self)) goto L02DF;
    damageAttitudeTo(instigatedBy);
    L02DF:
    PlayHit(actualDamage, HitLocation, DamageType, Momentum);
    goto L03A7;
    L02FD:
    if (bAlreadyDead) goto L038B;
    NextState = 'None';
    PlayDeathHit(actualDamage, HitLocation, DamageType, Momentum);
    if (actualDamage <= Mass) goto L0350;
    Health = -1 * actualDamage;
    L0350:
    if (!(instigatedBy != None && instigatedBy != self)) goto L0373;
    damageAttitudeTo(instigatedBy);
    L0373:
    Died(instigatedBy, DamageType, HitLocation);
    goto L03A7;
    L038B:
    if (bIsPlayer) goto L03A4;
    HidePlayer();
    GotoState('Dying');
    goto L03A7;
    L03A4:
    Destroy();
    L03A7:
    MakeNoise(1.0);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.Died

```unrealscript
function void Died(Killer: Pawn, DamageType: name, HitLocation: Vector)
{
    if (bDeleteMe) goto L000B;
    return;
    L000B:
    Health = Min(0, Health);
    OtherPawn = Level.PawnList;
    L002D:
    if (OtherPawn == None) goto L0069;
    OtherPawn.Killed(Killer, self, DamageType);
    OtherPawn = OtherPawn.nextPawn;
    goto L002D;
    L0069:
    if (carriedDecoration == None) goto L007A;
    DropDecoration();
    L007A:
    Level.Game.Killed(Killer, self, DamageType);
    if (Event == 'None') goto L00D8;
    foreach (AllActors(Actor, A, Event))  // end L00D7
    A.Trigger(self, Killer);
    continue;
    L00D7:
    L00D8:
    Level.Game.DiscardInventory(self);
    Velocity.Z *= 1.3;
    if (!(Gibbed(DamageType))) goto L012B;
    SpawnGibbedCarcass();
    if (bIsPlayer) goto L0128;
    HidePlayer();
    goto L012B;
    L0128:
    Destroy();
    L012B:
    PlayDying(DamageType, HitLocation);
    if (!(Level.Game.bGameEnded)) goto L0158;
    return;
    L0158:
    if (RemoteRole != 3) goto L0176;
    ClientDying(DamageType, HitLocation);
    L0176:
    GotoState('Dying');
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void Fire(Value: float)
{
    if (!(AmmoType.UseAmmo(1))) goto L00D2;
    GotoState('NormalFire');
    if (class'PlayerPawn'(Owner) == None) goto L004D;
    class'PlayerPawn'(Owner).ShakeView(shaketime, shakemag, shakevert);
    L004D:
    bPointing = true;
    PlayFiring();
    if (!(!bRapidFire && FiringSpeed > 0)) goto L008D;
    class'Pawn'(Owner).PlayRecoil(FiringSpeed);
    L008D:
    if (bInstantHit) goto L00A4;
    TraceFire(0.0);
    goto L00BA;
    L00A4:
    ProjectileFire(ProjectileClass, ProjectileSpeed, bWarnTarget);
    L00BA:
    if (!(Owner.bHidden)) goto L00D2;
    CheckVisibility();
    L00D2:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mover.InterpolateTo

```unrealscript
function void InterpolateTo(NewKeyNum: byte, Seconds: float)
{
    NewKeyNum = Clamp(NewKeyNum, 0, 8 - 1);
    if (!(NewKeyNum == PrevKeyNum && KeyNum != PrevKeyNum)) goto L0081;
    PhysAlpha = 1.0 - PhysAlpha;
    OldPos = BasePos + KeyPos[KeyNum];
    OldRot = BaseRot + KeyRot[KeyNum];
    goto L00A2;
    L0081:
    OldPos = Location;
    OldRot = Rotation;
    PhysAlpha = 0.0;
    L00A2:
    SetPhysics(9);
    bInterpolating = true;
    PrevKeyNum = KeyNum;
    KeyNum = NewKeyNum;
    PhysRate = 1.0 / FMax(Seconds, 0.005);
    ClientUpdate++;
    SimOldPos = OldPos;
    SimOldRotYaw = OldRot.Yaw;
    SimOldRotPitch = OldRot.Pitch;
    SimOldRotRoll = OldRot.Roll;
    SimInterpolate.X = 100.0 * PhysAlpha;
    SimInterpolate.Y = 100.0 * FMax(0.01, PhysRate);
    SimInterpolate.Z = 256.0 * PrevKeyNum + KeyNum;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ChangeTeam(N: int)

}
```

```unrealscript
    L0122:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ServerMove(TimeStamp: float, InAccel: Vector, ClientLoc: Vector, NewbRun: bool, NewbDuck: bool, NewbJumpStatus: bool, bFired: bool, bAltFired: bool, bForceFire: bool, bForceAltFire: bool, DodgeMove: EDodgeDir, ClientRoll: byte, View: int, OldTimeDelta: byte?, OldAccel: int?)
{

    return;
    L0011:
    if (OldTimeDelta == 0) goto L0234;
    OldTimeStamp = TimeStamp - OldTimeDelta / 500 - 0.001;
    if (CurrentTimeStamp >= OldTimeStamp - 0.001) goto L0234;
    Accel.X = OldAccel >>> 23;
    if (Accel.X <= 127) goto L009D;
    Accel.X = -1.0 * (Accel.X - 128);
    L009D:
    Accel.Y = OldAccel >>> 15 & 255;
    if (Accel.Y <= 127) goto L00EB;
    Accel.Y = -1.0 * (Accel.Y - 128);
    L00EB:
    Accel.Z = OldAccel >>> 7 & 255;
    if (Accel.Z <= 127) goto L0139;
    Accel.Z = -1.0 * (Accel.Z - 128);
    L0139:
    Accel *= 20;
    OldbRun = (OldAccel & 64) != 0;
    OldbDuck = (OldAccel & 32) != 0;
    NewbPressedJump = (OldAccel & 16) != 0;
    if (NewbPressedJump) goto L0192;
    bJumpStatus = NewbJumpStatus;
    L0192:
    switch (OldAccel & 7)
    case 0: goto L01AC;
    OldDodgeMove = 0;
    goto L01EE;
    L01AC:
    case 1: goto L01BB;
    OldDodgeMove = 1;
    goto L01EE;
    L01BB:
    case 2: goto L01CB;
    OldDodgeMove = 2;
    goto L01EE;
    L01CB:
    case 3: goto L01DB;
    OldDodgeMove = 3;
    goto L01EE;
    L01DB:
    case 4: goto L01EB;
    OldDodgeMove = 4;
    goto L01EE;
    L01EB:
    default:
    L01EE:
    PlayerPawn.MoveAutonomous(OldTimeStamp - CurrentTimeStamp, OldbRun, OldbDuck, NewbPressedJump, OldDodgeMove, Accel, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    CurrentTimeStamp = OldTimeStamp;
    L0234:
    ViewPitch = View / 32768;
    ViewYaw = 2 * (View - 32768 * ViewPitch);
    ViewPitch *= 2;
    Accel = InAccel / 10;
    NewbPressedJump = bJumpStatus != NewbJumpStatus;
    bJumpStatus = NewbJumpStatus;
    if (bFired) goto L02F2;
    if (!(bForceFire && Weapon != None)) goto L02D0;
    Weapon.ForceFire();
    goto L02E7;
    L02D0:
    if (bFire != 0) goto L02E7;
    Fire(0.0);
    L02E7:

    goto L02FA;
    L02F2:
    bFire = 0;
    L02FA:
    if (bAltFired) goto L034D;

    Weapon.ForceAltFire();

    L032B:

    L0342:
    bAltFire = 1;
    goto L0355;
    L034D:
    bAltFire = 0;
    L0355:
    DeltaTime = TimeStamp - CurrentTimeStamp;
    if (ServerTimeStamp <= 0) goto L03EB;
    TimeMargin += DeltaTime - 1.01 * (Level.TimeSeconds - ServerTimeStamp);
    if (TimeMargin <= MaxTimeMargin) goto L03EB;
    TimeMargin -= DeltaTime;
    if (TimeMargin >= 0.5) goto L03D5;
    MaxTimeMargin = default.MaxTimeMargin;
    goto L03E0;
    L03D5:
    MaxTimeMargin = 0.5;
    L03E0:
    DeltaTime = 0.0;
    L03EB:
    CurrentTimeStamp = TimeStamp;

}
```

```unrealscript
    L0147:
```

## Engine.Inventory.BotDesireability

```unrealscript
function float BotDesireability(Bot: Pawn)
{
    desire = MaxDesireability;
    if (RespawnTime >= 10) goto L0065;
    bChecked = true;
    AlreadyHas = Bot.FindInventoryType(Class);
    if (!(AlreadyHas != None && AlreadyHas.Charge >= Charge)) goto L0065;
    return -1.0;
    L0065:
    if (bIsAnArmor) goto L0100;
    if (bChecked) goto L0093;
    AlreadyHas = Bot.FindInventoryType(Class);
    L0093:
    if (AlreadyHas == None) goto L00CF;
    desire *= 1 - AlreadyHas.Charge * AlreadyHas.ArmorAbsorption * 0.00003;
    L00CF:
    desire *= Charge * 0.005;
    desire *= ArmorAbsorption * 0.01;
    return desire;
    goto L0106;
    L0100:
    return desire;
    L0106:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ActorEntered(Other: Actor)

    continue;
    L00C2:
    L00C3:
    if (!(bMoveProjectiles && ZoneVelocity != vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0))) goto L0164;

    AddEqual_VectorVector(Other.Velocity, ZoneVelocity);
    goto L0164;
    L0114:

    Other.SetPhysics(6);

    L0164:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.ChangedWeapon

```unrealscript
function void ChangedWeapon()
{
    OldWeapon = Weapon;
    if (Weapon != PendingWeapon) goto L0083;
    if (Weapon != None) goto L002E;
    SwitchToBestWeapon();
    goto L0051;
    L002E:
    if (!(Weapon.IsInState('DownWeapon'))) goto L0051;
    Weapon.BringUp();
    L0051:
    if (Weapon == None) goto L006B;
    Weapon.SetDefaultDisplayProperties();
    L006B:
    Inventory.ChangedWeapon();
    PendingWeapon = None;
    return;
    L0083:
    PlayWeaponSwitch(PendingWeapon);
    if (!(PendingWeapon != None && PendingWeapon.Mass > 20 && carriedDecoration != None)) goto L00C4;
    DropDecoration();
    L00C4:
    if (Weapon == None) goto L00DE;
    Weapon.SetDefaultDisplayProperties();
    L00DE:
    Weapon = PendingWeapon;
    Inventory.ChangedWeapon();
    if (Weapon == None) goto L016D;
    Weapon.RaiseUp(OldWeapon);
    if (!(Level.Game != None && Level.Game.Difficulty > 1)) goto L016D;
    MakeNoise(0.1 * Level.Game.Difficulty);
    L016D:
    PendingWeapon = None;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void SendVoiceMessage(Sender: PlayerReplicationInfo, Recipient: PlayerReplicationInfo, MessageType: name, MessageID: byte, broadcasttype: name)

    if (Level.TimeSeconds - OldMessageTime >= 2.5) goto L002A;
    bNoSpeak = true;
    goto L003E;
    L002A:
    OldMessageTime = Level.TimeSeconds;
    L003E:
    P = Level.PawnList;
    L0052:
    if (P == None) goto L0189;
    if (!(P.IsA('PlayerPawn'))) goto L0122;
    if (bNoSpeak) goto L011F;

    P.ClientVoiceMessage(Sender, Recipient, MessageType, MessageID);
    goto L011F;
    L00D0:
    if (Sender.Team != P.PlayerReplicationInfo.Team) goto L011F;
    P.ClientVoiceMessage(Sender, Recipient, MessageType, MessageID);
    L011F:
    goto L0172;
    L0122:
    if (!(P.PlayerReplicationInfo == Recipient || MessageType == 'ORDER' && Recipient == None)) goto L0172;
    P.BotVoiceMessage(MessageType, MessageID, self);
    L0172:
    P = P.nextPawn;
    goto L0052;
    L0189:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.PlayDying

```unrealscript
function void PlayDying(DamageType: name, HitLoc: Vector)
{
    if (Velocity.Z <= 250) goto L001F;
    PlayBigDeath(DamageType);
    return;
    L001F:
    if (DamageType != 'Decapitated') goto L003B;
    PlayHeadDeath(DamageType);
    return;
    L003B:
    GetAxes(Rotation, X, Y, Z);
    X.Z = 0.0;
    HitVec = Normal(HitLoc - Location);
    HitVec2D = HitVec;
    HitVec2D.Z = 0.0;
    dotp = Dot_VectorVector(HitVec2D, X);
    if (HitLoc.Z - Location.Z <= 0.5 * CollisionHeight) goto L00F0;
    if (dotp <= 0) goto L00E3;
    PlayHeadDeath(DamageType);
    goto L00EE;
    L00E3:
    PlayGutDeath(DamageType);
    L00EE:
    return;
    L00F0:
    if (dotp <= 0.71) goto L010D;
    PlayGutDeath(DamageType);
    goto L0147;
    L010D:
    dotp = Dot_VectorVector(HitVec, Y);
    if (dotp <= 0.0) goto L013C;
    PlayLeftDeath(DamageType);
    goto L0147;
    L013C:
    PlayRightDeath(DamageType);
    L0147:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.PlayTurning

```unrealscript

    L00C1:

    L00C4:
```

## Engine.Pawn.FootZoneChange

```unrealscript
function void FootZoneChange(newFootZone: ZoneInfo)
{
    if (Level.NetMode != 3) goto L0019;
    return;
    L0019:
    if (Level.TimeSeconds - SplashTime <= 0.25) goto L02A3;
    SplashTime = Level.TimeSeconds;
    if (Physics != 2) goto L0065;
    MakeNoise(1.0);
    goto L006D;
    L0065:
    MakeNoise(0.3);
    L006D:
    if (!(FootRegion.Zone.bWaterZone)) goto L012D;
    if (!(!newFootZone.bWaterZone && Role == 4)) goto L012A;
    if (FootRegion.Zone.ExitSound == None) goto L00DE;
    PlaySound(FootRegion.Zone.ExitSound, 3, 1.0);
    L00DE:
    if (FootRegion.Zone.ExitActor == None) goto L012A;
    Spawn(FootRegion.Zone.ExitActor, None, None, Location - CollisionHeight * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
    L012A:
    goto L02A3;
    L012D:
    if (!(newFootZone.bWaterZone && Role == 4)) goto L02A3;
    splashSize = FClamp(0.000025 * Mass * (300 - 0.5 * FMax(-500.0, Velocity.Z)), 1.0, 4.0);
    if (newFootZone.EntrySound == None) goto L023C;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, Location - (CollisionHeight + 40) * vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.8), Location - CollisionHeight * vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.8), false);
    if (HitActor != None) goto L021F;
    PlaySound(newFootZone.EntrySound, 1, 2.0 * splashSize);
    goto L023C;
    L021F:
    PlaySound(WaterStep, 1, 1.5 + 0.5 * splashSize);
    L023C:
    if (newFootZone.EntryActor == None) goto L02A3;
    splash = Spawn(newFootZone.EntryActor, None, None, Location - CollisionHeight * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
    if (splash == None) goto L02A3;
    splash.DrawScale = splashSize;
    L02A3:
    if (!(FootRegion.Zone.bPainZone)) goto L02F7;
    if (!(!newFootZone.bPainZone && !HeadRegion.Zone.bWaterZone)) goto L02F4;
    PainTime = -1.0;
    L02F4:
    goto L0314;
    L02F7:
    if (!(newFootZone.bPainZone)) goto L0314;
    PainTime = 0.01;
    L0314:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.PlayTakeHit

```unrealscript
function void PlayTakeHit(TweenTime: float, HitLoc: Vector, Damage: int)
{
    GetAxes(Rotation, X, Y, Z);
    X.Z = 0.0;
    HitVec = Normal(HitLoc - Location);
    HitVec2D = HitVec;
    HitVec2D.Z = 0.0;
    dotp = Dot_VectorVector(HitVec2D, X);
    if (HitLoc.Z - Location.Z <= 0.5 * CollisionHeight) goto L00B5;
    if (dotp <= 0) goto L00A8;
    PlayHeadHit(TweenTime);
    goto L00B3;
    L00A8:
    PlayGutHit(TweenTime);
    L00B3:
    return;
    L00B5:
    if (dotp <= 0.71) goto L00D2;
    PlayGutHit(TweenTime);
    goto L0129;
    L00D2:
    if (dotp >= -0.71) goto L00EF;
    PlayHeadHit(TweenTime);
    goto L0129;
    L00EF:
    dotp = Dot_VectorVector(HitVec, Y);
    if (dotp <= 0.0) goto L011E;
    PlayLeftHit(TweenTime);
    goto L0129;
    L011E:
    PlayRightHit(TweenTime);
    L0129:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ViewClass(aClass: class<Class>, bQuiet: bool?)
{
    if (!(Level.Game != None && !Level.Game.bCanViewOthers)) goto L0035;
    return;
    L0035:
    first = None;
    foreach (AllActors(aClass, Other))  // end L00D2
    if (!(first == None && Other != self && (bAdmin && Level.Game == None || Level.Game.CanSpectate(self, Other)))) goto L00BB;
    first = Other;
    bFound = true;
    L00BB:
    if (Other != ViewTarget) goto L00D1;
    first = None;
    L00D1:
    continue;
    L00D2:
    if (first == None) goto L0194;
    if (bQuiet) goto L0186;
    if (!(first.IsA('Pawn') && class'Pawn'(first).bIsPlayer && class'Pawn'(first).PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName != "")) goto L016D;
    ClientMessage(ViewingFrom @ class'Pawn'(first).PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName, 'Event', true);
    goto L0186;
    L016D:
    ClientMessage(ViewingFrom @ first, 'Event', true);
    L0186:
    ViewTarget = first;
    goto L01DB;
    L0194:
    if (bQuiet) goto L01D4;
    if (bFound) goto L01C3;
    ClientMessage(ViewingFrom @ OwnCamera, 'Event', true);
    goto L01D4;
    L01C3:
    ClientMessage(FailedView, 'Event', true);
    L01D4:
    ViewTarget = None;
    L01DB:
    bBehindView = ViewTarget != None;
    if (bBehindView) goto L0202;
    ViewTarget.BecomeViewTarget();
    L0202:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.UpdateEyeHeight

```unrealscript
function void UpdateEyeHeight(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (!(Physics == 1 && !bJustLanded)) goto L008A;
    smooth = FMin(1.0, 10.0 * DeltaTime / Level.TimeDilation);
    EyeHeight = (EyeHeight - Location.Z + OldLocation.Z) * (1 - smooth) + (shakevert + BaseEyeHeight) * smooth;
    goto L00EB;
    L008A:
    smooth = FClamp(10.0 * DeltaTime / Level.TimeDilation, 0.35, 1.0);
    bJustLanded = false;
    EyeHeight = EyeHeight * (1 - smooth) + (BaseEyeHeight + shakevert) * smooth;
    L00EB:
    if (FovAngle == DesiredFOV) goto L0189;
    if (FovAngle <= DesiredFOV) goto L013A;
    FovAngle = FovAngle - FMax(7.0, 0.9 * DeltaTime * (FovAngle - DesiredFOV));
    goto L0168;
    L013A:
    FovAngle = FovAngle - FMin(-7.0, 0.9 * DeltaTime * (FovAngle - DesiredFOV));
    L0168:
    if (Abs(FovAngle - DesiredFOV) > 10) goto L0189;
    FovAngle = DesiredFOV;
    L0189:
    if (bZooming) goto L01E4;
    ZoomLevel += DeltaTime * 1.0;
    if (ZoomLevel <= 0.9) goto L01BF;
    ZoomLevel = 0.9;
    L01BF:
    DesiredFOV = FClamp(90.0 - ZoomLevel * 88.0, 1.0, 170.0);
    L01E4:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerCalcView

```unrealscript
function void PlayerCalcView(out ViewActor: Actor, out CameraLocation: Vector, out CameraRotation: Rotator)
{
    if (ViewTarget == None) goto L0146;
    ViewActor = ViewTarget;
    CameraLocation = ViewTarget.Location;
    CameraRotation = ViewTarget.Rotation;
    PTarget = class'Pawn'(ViewTarget);
    if (PTarget == None) goto L0126;
    if (Level.NetMode != 3) goto L00DB;
    if (!(PTarget.bIsPlayer)) goto L0096;
    PTarget.ViewRotation = TargetViewRotation;
    L0096:
    PTarget.EyeHeight = TargetEyeHeight;
    if (PTarget.Weapon == None) goto L00DB;
    PTarget.Weapon.PlayerViewOffset = TargetWeaponViewOffset;
    L00DB:
    if (!(PTarget.bIsPlayer)) goto L0101;
    CameraRotation = PTarget.ViewRotation;
    L0101:
    if (bBehindView) goto L0126;
    CameraLocation.Z += PTarget.EyeHeight;
    L0126:
    if (bBehindView) goto L0144;
    CalcBehindView(CameraLocation, CameraRotation, 180.0);
    L0144:
    return;
    L0146:
    ViewActor = self;
    CameraLocation = Location;
    if (bBehindView) goto L0179;
    CalcBehindView(CameraLocation, CameraRotation, 150.0);
    goto L01A1;
    L0179:
    CameraRotation = ViewRotation;
    CameraLocation.Z += EyeHeight;
    CameraLocation += WalkBob;
    L01A1:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Fragment.HitWall

```unrealscript
function void HitWall(HitNormal: Vector, HitWall: Actor)
{
    Velocity = elasticity * (Dot_VectorVector(Velocity, HitNormal) * HitNormal * -2.0 + Velocity);
    speed = VSize(Velocity);

    bFirstHit = false;

    DesiredRotation.Yaw = FRand() * 65536;
    DesiredRotation.Roll = 0;
    L009C:
    RotationRate.Yaw = RotationRate.Yaw * 0.75;
    RotationRate.Roll = RotationRate.Roll * 0.75;
    RotationRate.Pitch = RotationRate.Pitch * 0.75;
    if (!(speed < 60 && HitNormal.Z > 0.7)) goto L0130;

    L01AC:

}
```

## Engine.Projectile.RandSpin

```unrealscript
function void RandSpin(spinRate: float)
{
    DesiredRotation = RotRand();
    RotationRate.Yaw = spinRate * 2 * FRand() - spinRate;
    RotationRate.Pitch = spinRate * 2 * FRand() - spinRate;
    RotationRate.Roll = spinRate * 2 * FRand() - spinRate;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void TakeDamage(Damage: int, instigatedBy: Pawn, HitLocation: Vector, Momentum: Vector, DamageType: name)

    Damage *= 0.4;
    L0083:

    ChunkUp(Damage);
    L0100:
    if (bDecorative) goto L011C;
    Velocity = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    L011C:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mover.HandleTriggerDoor

```unrealscript
function bool HandleTriggerDoor(Other: Pawn)
{

    WaitingPawn = Other;

    L0035:

    L0090:

    L0109:

    return false;
    L0159:
    Other.Target = self;
    Other.bShootSpecial = true;
    Other.FireWeapon();

    Other.bAltFire = 0;

    L01B9:

    Other.SpecialGoal = RecommendedTrigger;
    Other.MoveTarget = RecommendedTrigger;
    return true;
    L01EE:
    bOne = TriggerActor != None && (!TriggerActor.IsA('Trigger') || class'Trigger'(TriggerActor).IsRelevant(Other));
    bTwo = TriggerActor2 != None && (!TriggerActor2.IsA('Trigger') || class'Trigger'(TriggerActor2).IsRelevant(Other));
    if (!(bOne && bTwo)) goto L044E;
    Dist1 = VSize(TriggerActor.Location - Other.Location);
    Dist2 = VSize(TriggerActor2.Location - Other.Location);
    if (Dist1 >= Dist2) goto L0318;
    if (!(Dist1 < 500 && Other.actorReachable(TriggerActor))) goto L0315;
    bTwo = false;
    L0315:
    goto L0346;
    L0318:
    if (!(Dist2 < 500 && Other.actorReachable(TriggerActor2))) goto L0346;
    bOne = false;
    L0346:
    if (!(bOne && bTwo)) goto L044E;
    DP1 = Dot_VectorVector(Normal(Location - Other.Location), TriggerActor.Location - Other.Location) / Dist1;
    DP2 = Dot_VectorVector(Normal(Location - Other.Location), TriggerActor2.Location - Other.Location) / Dist2;
    if (!(DP1 > 0 && DP2 < 0)) goto L0407;
    bOne = false;
    goto L044E;
    L0407:
    if (!(DP1 < 0 && DP2 > 0)) goto L042C;
    bTwo = false;
    goto L044E;
    L042C:
    if (Dist1 >= Dist2) goto L0446;
    bTwo = false;
    goto L044E;
    L0446:
    bOne = false;
    L044E:
    if (bOne) goto L0484;
    Other.SpecialGoal = TriggerActor;
    Other.MoveTarget = TriggerActor;
    return true;
    goto L04B7;
    L0484:
    if (bTwo) goto L04B7;
    Other.SpecialGoal = TriggerActor2;
    Other.MoveTarget = TriggerActor2;
    return true;
    L04B7:
    return false;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ClientUpdatePosition()

    if (VSize(dist) >= AdjPCol + P.CollisionRadius + CollisionRadius + CurrentMove.Delta * GroundSpeed * Dot_VectorVector(Normal(Velocity), Normal(dist))) goto L025D;
    SavedPawn = P;
    SavedRadius = P.CollisionRadius;
    dist.Z = 0.0;
```

```unrealscript
function void ReplicateMove(DeltaTime: float, newAccel: Vector, DodgeMove: EDodgeDir, DeltaRot: Rotator)

    PendingMove.bAltFire = PendingMove.bAltFire || bJustAltFired || bAltFire != 0;

    OldMove = NewMove;
    L02D5:
    NewMove = NewMove.NextMove;
    goto L0236;
    L02EC:
    if (!(NewMove.bPressedJump || NewMove.DodgeMove != 0 && NewMove.DodgeMove < 5 || NewMove.Acceleration != newAccel && Dot_VectorVector(Normal(NewMove.Acceleration), AccelNorm) < 0.95)) goto L0377;
    OldMove = NewMove;
    L0377:
    LastMove = NewMove;

    L03C9:
    NewMove.Acceleration = newAccel;
    NewMove.DodgeMove = DodgeMove;
    NewMove.TimeStamp = Level.TimeSeconds;
    NewMove.bRun = bRun > 0;
    NewMove.bDuck = bDuck > 0;
    NewMove.bPressedJump = bPressedJump;
    NewMove.bFire = bJustFired || bFire != 0;
    NewMove.bForceFire = bJustFired;
    NewMove.bAltFire = bJustAltFired || bAltFire != 0;

    if (P == None) goto L06C1;
    if (!(P != self && P.Velocity != vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0) && P.bBlockPlayers)) goto L06AA;
    dist = P.Location - Location;
    AdjPCol = 0.0004 * PlayerReplicationInfo.Ping * Dot_VectorVector(P.Velocity - Velocity, Normal(dist));
    if (VSize(dist) >= AdjPCol + P.CollisionRadius + CollisionRadius + NewMove.Delta * GroundSpeed * Dot_VectorVector(Normal(Velocity), Normal(dist))) goto L06AA;
    SavedPawn = P;
    SavedRadius = P.CollisionRadius;
    dist.Z = 0.0;
    P.SetCollisionSize(FClamp(AdjPCol + P.CollisionRadius, 0.5 * P.CollisionRadius, VSize(dist) - CollisionRadius - P.CollisionRadius), P.CollisionHeight);
    goto L06C1;
    L06AA:
    P = P.nextPawn;
    goto L0520;
    L06C1:
    prevloc = Location;
    prevvelocity = Velocity;
    ProcessMove(NewMove.Delta, NewMove.Acceleration, NewMove.DodgeMove, DeltaRot * (NewMove.Delta / DeltaTime));
    AutonomousPhysics(NewMove.Delta);
    HighVelocityDelta = VelocityChanged(prevvelocity, Velocity);

    L0776:

    L078F:

    L07C8:
    NetMoveDelta = FMax(64.0 / Player.CurrentNetSpeed, 0.011);
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.CompressAccel

```unrealscript
function int CompressAccel(C: int)
{
    if (C < 0) goto L001D;
    C = Min(C, 127);
    goto L0034;
    L001D:
    C = Min(Abs(C), 127) + 128;
    L0034:
    return C;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mover.FindTriggerActor

```unrealscript
function void FindTriggerActor()
{
    TriggerActor = None;
    TriggerActor2 = None;
    foreach (AllActors(Actor, A))  // end L00C7
    if (!(A.Event == Tag && (A.IsA('Trigger') || A.IsA('Mover')))) goto L00C6;
    if (!(A.IsA('Counter') || A.IsA('Pawn'))) goto L0097;
    bPlayerOnly = true;
    return;
    L0097:
    if (TriggerActor != None) goto L00B0;
    TriggerActor = A;
    goto L00C6;
    L00B0:
    if (TriggerActor2 != None) goto L00C6;
    TriggerActor2 = A;
    L00C6:
    continue;
    L00C7:
    if (TriggerActor != None) goto L00E7;
    bPlayerOnly = BumpType == 0;
    return;
    L00E7:
    bPlayerOnly = TriggerActor.IsA('Trigger') && class'Trigger'(TriggerActor).TriggerType == 0;
    if (!(bPlayerOnly && TriggerActor2 != None)) goto L0195;
    bPlayerOnly = TriggerActor2.IsA('Trigger') && class'Trigger'(TriggerActor).TriggerType == 0;
    if (bPlayerOnly) goto L0195;
    A = TriggerActor;
    TriggerActor = TriggerActor2;
    TriggerActor2 = A;
    L0195:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.ClientAdjustPosition

```unrealscript
function void ClientAdjustPosition(TimeStamp: float, NewState: name, newPhysics: EPhysics, NewLocX: float, NewLocY: float, NewLocZ: float, NewVelX: float, NewVelY: float, NewVelZ: float, NewBase: Actor)

    NewLocation.Y = NewLocY;
    NewLocation.Z = NewLocZ;

    Velocity.Y = NewVelY;
    Velocity.Z = NewVelZ;

    NewLocation += NewBase.Location;
    L00A9:
    Carried = carriedDecoration;
    OldLoc = Location;
    bCanTeleport = false;
    SetLocation(NewLocation);
    bCanTeleport = true;
    if (Carried == None) goto L0130;
    carriedDecoration = Carried;
    carriedDecoration.SetLocation(NewLocation + carriedDecoration.Location - OldLoc);
    carriedDecoration.SetPhysics(0);

    L0130:
    SetPhysics(newPhysics);
    if (IsInState(NewState)) goto L014C;
    GotoState(NewState);
    L014C:
    bUpdatePosition = true;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.VelocityChanged

```unrealscript
function bool VelocityChanged(OldVel: Vector, NewVel: Vector)
{
    OldSize = VSize(OldVel);
    NewSize = VSize(NewVel);
    if (!(OldSize < 1 && NewSize < 1)) goto L0036;
    return false;
    L0036:
    if (OldSize <= 1.1 * NewSize) goto L004E;
    return true;
    L004E:
    if (NewSize <= 1.1 * OldSize) goto L0066;
    return true;
    L0066:
    NormDot = Dot_VectorVector(Normal(OldVel), Normal(NewVel));
    return NormDot < 0.99;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void HandleWalking()

    if (!(Role == 4 && StandingCount == 0)) goto L0069;

    DropDecoration();
    L010C:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ClientShake(shake: Vector)

    shakemag = shake.X;
    shaketimer = 0.01 * shake.Y;
    maxshake = 0.01 * shake.Z;
    verttimer = 0.0;
    shakevert = -1.1 * maxshake;
    L008E:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.KickBan

```unrealscript
function void KickBan(KickString: string)
{
    if (bAdmin) goto L000D;
    return;
    L000D:
    aPawn = Level.PawnList;
    L0021:
    if (aPawn == None) goto L01B9;
    if (!(aPawn.bIsPlayer && aPawn.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID != KickString && (class'PlayerPawn'(aPawn) == None || class'Engine.NetConnection'(class'PlayerPawn'(aPawn).Player) != None))) goto L01A2;
    IP = class'PlayerPawn'(aPawn).PlayerPawn.GetPlayerNetworkAddress();
    if (!(Level.Game.CheckIPPolicy(IP))) goto L0194;
    IP = Left(IP, InStr(IP, ":"));
    Log("Adding IP Ban for: " $ IP);
    j = 0;
    L010A:
    if (j >= 50) goto L0147;
    if (Level.Game.IPPolicies[j] != "") goto L013D;
    goto L0147;
    L013D:
    j++;
    goto L010A;
    L0147:
    if (j >= 50) goto L017F;
    Level.Game.IPPolicies[j] = "DENY," $ IP;
    L017F:
    Level.Game.SaveConfig();
    L0194:
    aPawn.Destroy();
    return;
    L01A2:
    aPawn = aPawn.nextPawn;
    goto L0021;
    L01B9:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.CheckBob

```unrealscript
function void CheckBob(DeltaTime: float, Speed2D: float, Y: Vector)
{
    OldBobTime = bobtime;
    if (Speed2D >= 10) goto L002E;
    bobtime += 0.2 * DeltaTime;
    goto L0056;
    L002E:
    bobtime += DeltaTime * (0.3 + 0.7 * Speed2D / GroundSpeed);
    L0056:
    WalkBob = Y * 0.65 * Bob * Speed2D * Sin(6.0 * bobtime);
    AppliedBob = AppliedBob * (1 - FMin(1.0, 16.0 * DeltaTime));
    if (LandBob <= 0.01) goto L00EF;
    AppliedBob += FMin(1.0, 16.0 * DeltaTime) * LandBob;
    LandBob *= 1 - 8 * DeltaTime;
    L00EF:
    if (Speed2D >= 10) goto L010F;
    WalkBob.Z = 0.0;
    goto L013D;
    L010F:
    WalkBob.Z = AppliedBob + Bob * Speed2D * Sin(12.0 * bobtime);
    L013D:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void Bump(Other: Actor)
```

```unrealscript
function void Typing(bTyping: bool)

    L009F:

    L00DF:

    Level.Game.LocalLog.LogTypingEvent(false, self);
    L011F:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
}
```

## Engine.Mover.DoOpen

```unrealscript
function void DoOpen()
{
    bOpening = true;
    bDelaying = false;
    Mover.InterpolateTo(1, MoveTime);
    PlaySound(OpeningSound, 0, TransientSoundVolume * SoundVolume / 255.0, None, 25.0 * (SoundRadius + 1));
    AmbientSound = MoveAmbientSound;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mover.DoClose

```unrealscript
function void DoClose()
{
    bOpening = false;
    bDelaying = false;
    Mover.InterpolateTo(Max(0, KeyNum - 1), MoveTime);
    PlaySound(ClosingSound, 0, TransientSoundVolume * SoundVolume / 255.0, None, 25.0 * (SoundRadius + 1));
    if (Event == 'None') goto L0093;
    foreach (AllActors(Actor, A, Event))  // end L0092
    A.UnTrigger(self, Instigator);
    continue;
    L0092:
    L0093:
    AmbientSound = MoveAmbientSound;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mover.BeginPlay

```unrealscript
function void BeginPlay()
{
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0053;
    if (Level.NetMode != 3) goto L003A;
    SetTimer(4.0, true);
    goto L0043;
    L003A:
    SetTimer(1.0, true);
    L0043:
    if (Role >= 4) goto L0053;
    return;
    L0053:
    if (Level.NetMode == 3) goto L0080;
    RealPosition = Location;
    RealRotation = Rotation;
    L0080:
    Actor.BeginPlay();
    KeyNum = Clamp(KeyNum, 0, 8 - 1);
    PhysAlpha = 0.0;
    Move(BasePos + KeyPos[KeyNum] - Location);
    SetRotation(BaseRot + KeyRot[KeyNum]);
    if (ReturnGroup != 'None') goto L00F4;
    ReturnGroup = Tag;
    L00F4:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mover.PostBeginPlay

```unrealscript
function void PostBeginPlay()
{
    if (Level.Game.IsRelevant(self)) goto L0046;
    SetCollision(false, false, false);
    SetLocation(Location + vect(0.0, 0.0, 20000.0));
    bHidden = true;
    goto L0115;
    L0046:
    FindTriggerActor();
    if (bSlave) goto L009D;
    foreach (AllActors(Mover, M, Tag))  // end L009C
    if (!(M.bSlave)) goto L009B;
    M.GotoState('None');
    M.SetBase(self);
    L009B:
    continue;
    L009C:
    L009D:
    if (Leader != None) goto L0115;
    Leader = self;
    foreach (AllActors(Mover, M))  // end L0114
    if (!(M != self && M.ReturnGroup == ReturnGroup)) goto L0113;
    M.Leader = self;
    M.Follower = Follower;
    Follower = M;
    L0113:
    continue;
    L0114:
    L0115:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
    PendingWeapon = W;
    L01CC:
```

```unrealscript
    continue;
    L009C:
    L009D:

    foreach (AllActors(GameReplicationInfo, G))  // end L00E6
    PlaybackGRI = G;

    PlaybackActor.GameReplicationInfo = PlaybackGRI;
    L00E2:
    goto L00E6;
    continue;
    L00E6:
    L00E7:

    Disable('Tick');
    L0106:
    goto L0110;
    L0109:
    Disable('Tick');
    L0110:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function bool EncroachingOn(Other: Actor)
{
    if (!(Other.IsA('Carcass') || Other.IsA('Decoration'))) goto L0061;
    Other.TakeDamage(10000, None, Other.Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 'Crushed');
    return false;
    L0061:

    Other.Destroy();
    return false;
    L0083:

    return false;
    L0099:
    if (EncroachDamage == 0) goto L00DD;
    Other.TakeDamage(EncroachDamage, Instigator, Other.Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 'Crushed');
    L00DD:
    P = class'Pawn'(Other);
    if (!(P != None && P.bIsPlayer)) goto L01AB;
    if (PlayerBumpEvent == 'None') goto L0126;
    Bump(Other);
    L0126:
    if (!(myMarker != None && P.Base != self && P.Location.Z < myMarker.Location.Z - P.CollisionHeight - 0.7 * myMarker.CollisionHeight)) goto L01AB;
    P.UnderLift(self);
    L01AB:
    if (MoverEncroachType != 0) goto L01CD;
    Leader.MakeGroupStop();
    return true;
    goto L02A0;
    L01CD:
    if (MoverEncroachType != 1) goto L0269;
    Leader.MakeGroupReturn();
    if (!(Other.IsA('Pawn'))) goto L0264;
    if (!(class'Pawn'(Other).bIsPlayer)) goto L023E;
    class'Pawn'(Other).PlaySound(class'Pawn'(Other).Land, 0);
    goto L0264;
    L023E:
    class'Pawn'(Other).PlaySound(class'Pawn'(Other).HitSound1, 0);

    return true;
```

## Engine.GameReplicationInfo.Timer

```unrealscript
function void Timer()
{
    if (Level.NetMode != 3) goto L0097;
    if (Level.TimeSeconds - SecondCount < Level.TimeDilation) goto L0097;
    ElapsedTime++;
    if (RemainingMinute == 0) goto L0063;
    RemainingTime = RemainingMinute;
    RemainingMinute = 0;
    L0063:
    if (!(RemainingTime > 0 && !bStopCountDown)) goto L0082;
    RemainingTime--;
    L0082:
    SecondCount += Level.TimeDilation;
    L0097:
    i = 0;
    L009E:
    if (i >= 32) goto L00C1;
    PRIArray[i] = None;
    i++;
    goto L009E;
    L00C1:
    i = 0;
    foreach (AllActors(PlayerReplicationInfo, PRI))  // end L00EC
    PRIArray[i++] = PRI;
    continue;
    L00EC:
    UpdateTimer = 0.0;
    i = 0;
    L00FF:
    if (i >= 32) goto L0142;
    if (PRIArray[i] == None) goto L0138;
    FragAcc += PRIArray[i].Score;
    L0138:
    i++;
    goto L00FF;
    L0142:
    SumFrags = FragAcc;
    if (Level.Game == None) goto L017E;
    NumPlayers = Level.Game.NumPlayers;
    L017E:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.SwitchToBestWeapon

```unrealscript
function bool SwitchToBestWeapon()
{
    if (Inventory != None) goto L000D;
    return false;
    L000D:
    PendingWeapon = Inventory.RecommendWeapon(rating, usealt);
    if (PendingWeapon != Weapon) goto L0042;
    PendingWeapon = None;
    L0042:
    if (PendingWeapon != None) goto L004F;
    return false;
    L004F:
    if (Weapon != None) goto L0060;
    ChangedWeapon();
    L0060:
    if (Weapon == PendingWeapon) goto L007E;
    Weapon.PutDown();
    L007E:
    return usealt > 0;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerSpectating.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    GetAxes(ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    aForward *= 0.1;
    aStrafe *= 0.1;
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    aUp *= 0.1;
    Acceleration = aForward * X + aStrafe * Y + aUp * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    UpdateRotation(DeltaTime, 1.0);
    if (Role >= 4) goto L00C8;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, Acceleration, 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    goto L00E7;
    L00C8:
    ProcessMove(DeltaTime, Acceleration, 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    L00E7:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.GameEnded.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    GetAxes(ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    if (bFixedCamera) goto L00F1;
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    ViewRotation.Yaw += 32.0 * DeltaTime * aTurn;
    ViewRotation.Pitch += 32.0 * DeltaTime * aLookUp;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = ViewRotation.Pitch & 65535;
    if (!(ViewRotation.Pitch > 18000 && ViewRotation.Pitch < 49152)) goto L00EE;
    if (aLookUp <= 0) goto L00DE;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 18000;
    goto L00EE;
    L00DE:
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 49152;
    L00EE:
    goto L0110;
    L00F1:
    if (ViewTarget == None) goto L0110;
    ViewRotation = ViewTarget.Rotation;
    L0110:
    ViewShake(DeltaTime);
    ViewFlash(DeltaTime);
    if (Role >= 4) goto L015E;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    goto L0185;
    L015E:
    ProcessMove(DeltaTime, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    L0185:
    bPressedJump = false;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.StatLog.GetShortAbsoluteTime

```unrealscript
function string GetShortAbsoluteTime()
{
    AbsoluteTime = Level.Year;
    if (Level.Month >= 10) goto L004F;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Month;
    goto L0070;
    L004F:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Month;
    L0070:
    if (Level.Day >= 10) goto L00AA;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Day;
    goto L00CB;
    L00AA:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Day;
    L00CB:
    if (Level.Hour >= 10) goto L0105;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Hour;
    goto L0126;
    L0105:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Hour;
    L0126:
    if (Level.Minute >= 10) goto L0160;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Minute;
    goto L0181;
    L0160:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Minute;
    L0181:
    if (Level.Second >= 10) goto L01BB;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Second;
    goto L01DC;
    L01BB:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Second;
    L01DC:
    TimeStamp = 0.0;
    return AbsoluteTime;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.GameEnded.FindGoodView

```unrealscript
function void FindGoodView()
{
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 56000;
    tries = 0;
    besttry = 0;
    bestDist = 0.0;
    startYaw = ViewRotation.Yaw;
    tries = 0;
    L0040:
    if (tries >= 16) goto L00E2;
    if (ViewTarget == None) goto L006E;
    cameraLoc = ViewTarget.Location;
    goto L0079;
    L006E:
    cameraLoc = Location;
    L0079:
    PlayerCalcView(ViewActor, cameraLoc, cameraRot);
    newdist = VSize(cameraLoc - Location);
    if (newdist <= bestDist) goto L00C7;
    bestDist = newdist;
    besttry = tries;
    L00C7:
    ViewRotation.Yaw += 4096;
    tries++;
    goto L0040;
    L00E2:
    ViewRotation.Yaw = startYaw + besttry * 4096;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.CheatFlying.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    GetAxes(ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    aForward *= 0.1;
    aStrafe *= 0.1;
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    aUp *= 0.1;
    Acceleration = aForward * X + aStrafe * Y + aUp * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    UpdateRotation(DeltaTime, 1.0);
    if (Role >= 4) goto L00C8;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, Acceleration, 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    goto L00E7;
    L00C8:
    ProcessMove(DeltaTime, Acceleration, 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    L00E7:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void Bump(Other: Actor)

}
```

## Engine.StatLogFile.StartLog

```unrealscript
function void StartLog()
{
    SaveConfig();
    AbsoluteTime = GetShortAbsoluteTime();
    if (bWorld) goto L0092;
    FileName = LocalLogDir $ "/" $ GameName $ "." $ LocalStandard $ "." $ AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Game.GetServerPort();
    StatLogFile = FileName $ ".tmp";
    StatLogFinal = FileName $ ".log";
    goto L010F;
    L0092:
    FileName = WorldLogDir $ "/" $ GameName $ "." $ WorldStandard $ "." $ AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Game.GetServerPort();
    StatLogFile = FileName $ ".tmp";
    StatLogFinal = FileName $ ".log";
    bWatermark = true;
    L010F:
    StatLogFile.OpenLog();
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.TakeFallingDamage

```unrealscript
function void TakeFallingDamage()
{
    if (Velocity.Z >= -1.4 * JumpZ) goto L013C;
    MakeNoise(-0.5 * Velocity.Z / FMax(JumpZ, 150.0));
    if (Velocity.Z > -750 - JumpZ) goto L0139;
    if (!(Velocity.Z < -1650 - JumpZ && ReducedDamageType != 'All')) goto L00AC;
    TakeDamage(1000, None, Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 'Fell');
    goto L00F9;
    L00AC:
    if (Role != 4) goto L00F9;
    TakeDamage(-0.15 * (Velocity.Z + 700 + JumpZ), None, Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 'Fell');
    L00F9:
    ShakeView(0.175 - 0.00007 * Velocity.Z, -0.85 * Velocity.Z, -0.002 * Velocity.Z);
    L0139:
    goto L015F;
    L013C:
    if (Velocity.Z <= 0.5 * default.JumpZ) goto L015F;
    MakeNoise(0.35);
    L015F:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.SpawnNotify.Destroyed

```unrealscript
function Actor TraceShot(out HitLocation: Vector, out HitNormal: Vector, EndTrace: Vector, StartTrace: Vector)
{
    Other = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, EndTrace, StartTrace, true);
    if (class'Pawn'(Other) == None) goto L0091;
    realHit = HitLocation;
    if (class'Pawn'(Other).AdjustHitLocation(HitLocation, EndTrace - StartTrace)) goto L0091;
    Other = class'Pawn'(Other).TraceShot(HitLocation, HitNormal, EndTrace, realHit);
    L0091:
    return Other;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void FindGoodView()
{
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 56000;
    tries = 0;
    besttry = 0;
    bestDist = 0.0;
    startYaw = ViewRotation.Yaw;
    tries = 0;
    L0040:
    if (tries >= 16) goto L00C0;
    cameraLoc = Location;
    PlayerCalcView(ViewActor, cameraLoc, cameraRot);
    newdist = VSize(cameraLoc - Location);
    if (newdist <= bestDist) goto L00A5;
    bestDist = newdist;
    besttry = tries;
    L00A5:
    ViewRotation.Yaw += 4096;
    tries++;
    goto L0040;
    L00C0:
    ViewRotation.Yaw = startYaw + besttry * 4096;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.Dying.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (bFrozen) goto L013F;
    if (bPressedJump) goto L0027;
    Fire(0.0);
    bPressedJump = false;
    L0027:
    GetAxes(ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    ViewRotation.Yaw += 32.0 * DeltaTime * aTurn;
    ViewRotation.Pitch += 32.0 * DeltaTime * aLookUp;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = ViewRotation.Pitch & 65535;
    if (!(ViewRotation.Pitch > 18000 && ViewRotation.Pitch < 49152)) goto L010A;
    if (aLookUp <= 0) goto L00FA;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 18000;
    goto L010A;
    L00FA:
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 49152;
    L010A:
    if (Role >= 4) goto L013F;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    L013F:
    ViewShake(DeltaTime);
    ViewFlash(DeltaTime);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.WarpZoneInfo.ActorEntered

```unrealscript
function void ActorEntered(Other: Actor)
{
    ZoneInfo.ActorEntered(Other);
    if (Other.bJustTeleported) goto L024E;
    Generate();
    if (OtherSideActor == None) goto L024E;
    Other.Disable('Touch');
    Other.Disable('UnTouch');
    L = Other.Location;
    if (!(Other.IsA('PlayerPawn'))) goto L0094;
    R = class'PlayerPawn'(Other).ViewRotation;
    goto L00A8;
    L0094:
    R = Other.Rotation;
    L00A8:
    UnWarp(L, Other.Velocity, R);
    OtherSideActor.Warp(L, Other.Velocity, R);
    if (!(Other.IsA('Pawn'))) goto L020C;
    class'Pawn'(Other).bWarping = bNoTeleFrag;
    if (!(Other.SetLocation(L))) goto L0202;
    if (Role != 4) goto L01A3;
    P = Level.PawnList;
    L014C:
    if (P == None) goto L01A3;
    if (P.Enemy != Other) goto L018C;
    P.LastSeenPos = Other.Location;
    L018C:
    P = P.nextPawn;
    goto L014C;
    L01A3:
    R.Roll = 0;
    class'Pawn'(Other).ViewRotation = R;
    class'Pawn'(Other).ClientSetLocation(L, R);
    class'Pawn'(Other).MoveTimer = -1.0;
    goto L0209;
    L0202:
    GotoState('DelayedWarp');
    L0209:
    goto L022E;
    L020C:
    Other.SetLocation(L);
    Other.SetRotation(R);
    L022E:
    Other.Enable('Touch');
    Other.Enable('UnTouch');
    L024E:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void LookAtVector(lookTo: Vector, bRotate: bool, bLookHorizontal: bool, bLookVertical: bool, DelayTime: float?, Rate: float?, LockAngle: float?, AngleOffset: float?)

    if (Rate > 0) goto L0017;
    Rate = 1.0;
    L0017:
    hAngle = 5461;
    vAngle = 2731;

    lookAngle = lookTo - lookFrom;
    lookAngle.Yaw = lookAngle.Yaw - Rotation.Yaw & 65535;
    if (lookAngle.Yaw <= 32767) goto L00B5;
    lookAngle.Yaw -= 65536;
    L00B5:
    if (lookAngle.Pitch <= 32767) goto L00DA;
    lookAngle.Pitch -= 65536;
    L00DA:

    vPos = 0;

    if (lookAngle.Pitch <= vAngle * 0.9) goto L0118;
    vPos = 1;
    goto L0142;
    L0118:
    if (lookAngle.Pitch >= -vAngle * 0.75) goto L0142;
    vPos = -1;
    L0142:
    if (bLookHorizontal) goto L019C;
    if (lookAngle.Yaw <= hAngle * 0.5) goto L0172;
    hPos = 1;
    goto L019C;
    L0172:
    if (lookAngle.Yaw >= -hAngle * 0.5) goto L019C;
    hPos = -1;
    L019C:
    hAbs = Abs(lookAngle.Yaw);
    vAbs = Abs(lookAngle.Pitch);
    if (bRotate) goto L0410;
    hRot = lookAngle.Yaw;
    if (!(bLookHorizontal && vPos == 0)) goto L025F;
    if (hRot <= hAngle * 1.2) goto L0223;
    hRot -= hAngle * 1.2;
    goto L025C;
    L0223:
    if (hRot >= -hAngle * 1.2) goto L0255;
    hRot += hAngle * 1.2;
    goto L025C;
    L0255:
    hRot = 0;
    L025C:
    goto L02DE;
    L025F:
    if (!(bLookVertical && hPos == 0)) goto L02DE;
    if (hRot <= hAngle * 0.35) goto L02A5;
    hRot -= hAngle * 0.35;
    goto L02DE;
    L02A5:
    if (hRot >= -hAngle * 0.35) goto L02D7;
    hRot += hAngle * 0.35;
    goto L02DE;
    L02D7:
    hRot = 0;
    L02DE:

    hRot = hRot + (Rotation.Yaw - LockAngle) + 65536 * 4 & 65535;
    if (hRot <= 32767) goto L0339;
    hRot -= 65536;
    L0339:

    hRot = -AngleOffset;
    goto L0378;
    L035C:
    if (hRot <= AngleOffset) goto L0378;
    hRot = AngleOffset;
    L0378:
    hRot = hRot + (LockAngle - Rotation.Yaw) + 65536 * 4 & 65535;
    if (hRot <= 32767) goto L03C7;
    hRot -= 65536;
    L03C7:
    hAbs = 65536 + lookAngle.Yaw - hRot & 65535;
    if (hAbs <= 32767) goto L040D;
    hAbs = 65536 - hAbs;
    L040D:
    goto L0417;
    L0410:
    hRot = 0;
    L0417:
    if (!(hPos != 0 && vPos != 0)) goto L044F;
    if (hAbs <= vAbs) goto L0448;
    vPos = 0;
    goto L044F;
    L0448:
    hPos = 0;
    L044F:
    if (hPos <= 0) goto L0465;
    lookDir = 2;
    goto L04AF;
    L0465:
    if (hPos >= 0) goto L047B;
    lookDir = 1;
    goto L04AF;
    L047B:

    L0491:

    L04A7:
```

```unrealscript
    L011B:
    if (class'Carcass'(ViewTarget) == None) goto L0187;

    CameraRotation = ViewRotation;
    goto L0166;
    L015B:

    L01A7:

    goto L0201;
    L01E5:
    CameraLocation = Location;
    CameraLocation.Z += default.BaseEyeHeight;
    L0201:
    return;
```

## Engine.Pawn.LoopBaseConvoAnim

```unrealscript
function void LoopBaseConvoAnim()
{
    rnd = FRand();
    if (bIsSpeaking) goto L009A;
    if (animTimer[2] <= 2.5) goto L009A;
    animTimer[2] = 0.0;
    if (rnd >= 0.1) goto L0055;
    PlayAnim('GestureLeft', 0.35, 0.4);
    goto L009A;
    L0055:
    if (rnd >= 0.2) goto L0079;
    PlayAnim('GestureRight', 0.35, 0.4);
    goto L009A;
    L0079:
    if (rnd >= 0.3) goto L009A;
    PlayAnim('GestureBoth', 0.35, 0.4);
    L009A:
    if (IsAnimating()) goto L00B0;
    LoopAnim('BreatheLight', None, 0.4);
    L00B0:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.LoopHeadConvoAnim

```unrealscript
function void LoopHeadConvoAnim()
{
    rnd = FRand();
    if (!(!bIsSpeaking && animTimer[1] > 0.5)) goto L0080;
    if (rnd >= 0.01) goto L004A;
    PlayTurnHead(1, 1.0, 2.0);
    goto L0080;
    L004A:
    if (rnd >= 0.02) goto L006E;
    PlayTurnHead(2, 1.0, 2.0);
    goto L0080;
    L006E:
    PlayTurnHead(0, 1.0, 1.0);
    L0080:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void InitAllPoints()

    Rot.Roll = Rotation.Roll % 65536;

    L0128:

}
```

```unrealscript
function bool PlayTurnHead(Dir: ELookDirection, Rate: float, TweenTime: float)

    L001C:
    if (Dir != 2) goto L0038;

    goto L007B;
    L0038:
    if (Dir != 3) goto L0054;
    lookName = 'HeadUp';
    goto L007B;
    L0054:

    L0070:
    lookName = 'Still';
    L007B:
    bSuccess = false;

    if (BlendAnimSequence[3] != 'None') goto L00D3;
    BlendAnimSequence[3] = 'Still';
    L00D3:
    PlayBlendAnim(lookName, Rate, TweenTime, 3);
    bSuccess = true;
    L00EF:
    return bSuccess;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.LipSynch

```unrealscript
function void LipSynch(DeltaTime: float)
{
    animTimer[0] += DeltaTime;
    animTimer[1] += DeltaTime;
    animTimer[2] += DeltaTime;
    if (bIsSpeaking) goto L0194;
    if (Level.TimeSeconds - animTimer[3] >= 0.05) goto L0064;
    TweenTime = 0.1;
    goto L006F;
    L0064:
    TweenTime = 0.0;
    L006F:
    animTimer[3] = Level.TimeSeconds;
    if (nextPhoneme != "A") goto L00A1;
    animseq = 'MouthA';
    goto L015B;
    L00A1:
    if (nextPhoneme != "E") goto L00BC;
    animseq = 'MouthE';
    goto L015B;
    L00BC:
    if (nextPhoneme != "F") goto L00D7;
    animseq = 'MouthF';
    goto L015B;
    L00D7:
    if (nextPhoneme != "M") goto L00F2;
    animseq = 'MouthM';
    goto L015B;
    L00F2:
    if (nextPhoneme != "O") goto L010D;
    animseq = 'MouthO';
    goto L015B;
    L010D:
    if (nextPhoneme != "T") goto L0128;
    animseq = 'MouthT';
    goto L015B;
    L0128:
    if (nextPhoneme != "U") goto L0143;
    animseq = 'MouthU';
    goto L015B;
    L0143:
    if (nextPhoneme != "X") goto L015B;
    animseq = 'MouthClosed';
    L015B:
    if (animseq == 'None') goto L0191;
    if (lastPhoneme == nextPhoneme) goto L0191;
    lastPhoneme = nextPhoneme;
    TweenBlendAnim(animseq, TweenTime);
    L0191:
    goto L01B2;
    L0194:
    if (bWasSpeaking) goto L01B2;
    bWasSpeaking = false;
    TweenBlendAnim('MouthClosed', TweenTime);
    L01B2:
    if (animTimer[0] <= 2.0) goto L01EF;
    animTimer[0] = 0.0;
    if (FRand() >= 0.4) goto L01EF;
    PlayBlendAnim('Blink', 1.0, 0.1, 1);
    L01EF:
    LoopHeadConvoAnim();
    LoopBaseConvoAnim();
    return;
}
```

## Engine.CameraPoint.Running.Tick

```unrealscript
function void Tick(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (bTickReady) goto L000D;
    return;
    L000D:
    Actor.Tick(DeltaTime);
    curTime += DeltaTime;
    if (curTime < timeSmooth) goto L0089;
    Player.ViewRotation = endRot;
    Player.SetLocation(endLoc);
    Player.SetFOVAngle(endFOV);
    Player.DesiredFOV = endFOV;
    Next();
    goto L02B4;
    L0089:
    Alpha = curTime / timeSmooth;
    diffrot = endRot - startRot;
    if (diffrot.Pitch < 32768) goto L00E1;
    diffrot.Pitch = diffrot.Pitch - 65536;
    goto L0111;
    L00E1:
    if (diffrot.Pitch > -32768) goto L0111;
    diffrot.Pitch = diffrot.Pitch + 65536;
    L0111:
    if (diffrot.Yaw < 32768) goto L0144;
    diffrot.Yaw = diffrot.Yaw - 65536;
    goto L0174;
    L0144:
    if (diffrot.Yaw > -32768) goto L0174;
    diffrot.Yaw = diffrot.Yaw + 65536;
    L0174:
    if (diffrot.Roll < 32768) goto L01A7;
    diffrot.Roll = diffrot.Roll - 65536;
    goto L01D7;
    L01A7:
    if (diffrot.Roll > -32768) goto L01D7;
    diffrot.Roll = diffrot.Roll + 65536;
    L01D7:
    beta = 3.0 * Alpha * Alpha - 2.0 * Alpha * Alpha * Alpha;
    Rot = startRot + beta * diffrot;
    Loc = startLoc + beta * (endLoc - startLoc);
    FOV = startFOV + beta * (endFOV - startFOV);
    Player.SetLocation(Loc);
    Player.ViewRotation = Rot;
    Player.SetFOVAngle(FOV);
    Player.DesiredFOV = FOV;
    L02B4:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerSwimming.ProcessMove

```unrealscript
function void ProcessMove(DeltaTime: float, newAccel: Vector, DodgeMove: EDodgeDir, DeltaRot: Rotator)
{
    GetAxes(ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    Acceleration = newAccel;
    SwimAnimUpdate(Dot_VectorVector(X, Acceleration) <= 0);
    bUpAndOut = Dot_VectorVector(X, Acceleration) > 0 && Acceleration.Z > 0 && ViewRotation.Pitch > 4096;
    if (!(bUpAndOut && !Region.Zone.bWaterZone && CheckWaterJump(Temp))) goto L00D4;
    Velocity.Z = 330.0 + 2 * CollisionRadius;
    PlayDuck();
    GotoState('PlayerWalking');
    L00D4:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerSwimming.ZoneChange

```unrealscript
function void ZoneChange(NewZone: ZoneInfo)
{
    if (NewZone.bWaterZone) goto L010A;
    SetPhysics(2);
    if (!(bUpAndOut && CheckWaterJump(HitNormal))) goto L005E;
    Velocity.Z = 330.0 + 2 * CollisionRadius;
    PlayDuck();
    GotoState('PlayerWalking');
    goto L0107;
    L005E:
    if (!(!FootRegion.Zone.bWaterZone || Velocity.Z > 160)) goto L009B;
    GotoState('PlayerWalking');
    AnimEnd();
    goto L0107;
    L009B:
    checkpoint = Location;
    checkpoint.Z -= CollisionHeight + 6.0;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, checkpoint, Location, false);
    if (HitActor == None) goto L00F7;
    GotoState('PlayerWalking');
    AnimEnd();
    goto L0107;
    L00F7:
    Enable('Timer');
    SetTimer(0.7, false);
    L0107:
    goto L0116;
    L010A:
    Disable('Timer');
    SetPhysics(3);
    L0116:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void WakeUpRandomPoint()
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerSwimming.UpdateEyeHeight

```unrealscript
function void UpdateEyeHeight(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (bJustLanded) goto L00F2;
    smooth = FMin(1.0, 10.0 * DeltaTime / Level.TimeDilation);
    EyeHeight = (EyeHeight - Location.Z + OldLocation.Z) * (1 - smooth) + (shakevert + BaseEyeHeight) * smooth;
    bound = -0.5 * CollisionHeight;
    if (EyeHeight >= bound) goto L00A6;
    EyeHeight = bound;
    goto L00EF;
    L00A6:
    bound = CollisionHeight + FClamp(OldLocation.Z - Location.Z, 0.0, MaxStepHeight);
    if (EyeHeight <= bound) goto L00EF;
    EyeHeight = bound;
    L00EF:
    goto L0153;
    L00F2:
    smooth = FClamp(10.0 * DeltaTime / Level.TimeDilation, 0.35, 1.0);
    bJustLanded = false;
    EyeHeight = EyeHeight * (1 - smooth) + (BaseEyeHeight + shakevert) * smooth;
    L0153:
    if (FovAngle == DesiredFOV) goto L01F1;
    if (FovAngle <= DesiredFOV) goto L01A2;
    FovAngle = FovAngle - FMax(7.0, 0.9 * DeltaTime * (FovAngle - DesiredFOV));
    goto L01D0;
    L01A2:
    FovAngle = FovAngle - FMin(-7.0, 0.9 * DeltaTime * (FovAngle - DesiredFOV));
    L01D0:
    if (Abs(FovAngle - DesiredFOV) > 10) goto L01F1;
    FovAngle = DesiredFOV;
    L01F1:
    if (bZooming) goto L024C;
    ZoomLevel += DeltaTime * 1.0;
    if (ZoomLevel <= 0.9) goto L0227;
    ZoomLevel = 0.9;
    L0227:
    DesiredFOV = FClamp(90.0 - ZoomLevel * 88.0, 1.0, 170.0);
    L024C:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function bool EvaluateCommand()
{
    Loc = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    curTime = 0.0;
    switch (Cmd)
    case 0: goto L0043;
    endLoc = Location;
    endRot = Rotation;

}
```

## Engine.InterpolationPoint.BeginPlay

```unrealscript
function void BeginPlay()
{
    Actor.BeginPlay();
    foreach (AllActors(InterpolationPoint, Prev, Tag))  // end L003A
    if (Prev.Position != Position - 1) goto L0039;
    goto L003A;
    L0039:
    continue;
    L003A:
    if (Prev == None) goto L0056;
    Prev.Next = self;
    L0056:
    foreach (AllActors(InterpolationPoint, Next, Tag))  // end L008A
    if (Next.Position != Position + 1) goto L0089;
    goto L008A;
    L0089:
    continue;
    L008A:
    if (Next != None) goto L00C4;
    foreach (AllActors(InterpolationPoint, Next, Tag))  // end L00C3
    if (Next.Position != 0) goto L00C2;
    goto L00C3;
    L00C2:
    continue;
    L00C3:
    L00C4:
    if (Next == None) goto L00DF;
    Next.Prev = self;
    L00DF:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void InitLogging()

    LocalLog.LogMapParameters();

    return;
    L0121:
    if (!(bWorldLog && bLoggingGame)) goto L0220;
    Log("Initiating world logging...");
    WorldLog = Spawn(StatLogClass);
    WorldLog.bWorld = true;
    WorldLog.StartLog();
    WorldLog.LogStandardInfo();

    WorldLog.LogMapParameters();
    WorldLog.StatLog.InitialCheck(self);

    M = M.NextMutator;
    goto L01CA;
    L0200:
    LogGameParameters(WorldLog);
    WorldLogFileName = WorldLog.GetLogFileName();
    L0220:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function bool IsRelevant(Other: Actor)

    return false;

    return false;
    L01B8:

    return false;
    L01CF:

    L01E8:

    ItemGoals++;
    L0201:

    KillGoals++;
    L021A:
    return true;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.InitGame

```unrealscript
function void InitGame(Options: string, out Error: string)
{
    Log("InitGame:" @ Options);
    MaxPlayers = Min(32, GetIntOption(Options, "MaxPlayers", MaxPlayers));
    InOpt = ParseOption(Options, "Difficulty");
    if (InOpt == "") goto L0070;
    Difficulty = InOpt;
    L0070:
    InOpt = ParseOption(Options, "AdminPassword");
    if (InOpt == "") goto L00A7;
    AdminPassword = InOpt;
    L00A7:
    InOpt = ParseOption(Options, "GameSpeed");
    if (InOpt == "") goto L00EF;
    Log("GameSpeed" @ InOpt);
    SetGameSpeed(InOpt);
    L00EF:
    BaseMutator = Spawn(MutatorClass);
    Log("Base Mutator is " $ BaseMutator);
    InOpt = ParseOption(Options, "Mutator");
    if (InOpt == "") goto L020B;
    Log("Mutators" @ InOpt);
    L0152:
    if (InOpt == "") goto L020B;
    pos = InStr(InOpt, ",");
    if (pos <= 0) goto L01AC;
    LeftOpt = Left(InOpt, pos);
    InOpt = Right(InOpt, Len(InOpt) - pos - 1);
    goto L01BF;
    L01AC:
    LeftOpt = InOpt;
    InOpt = "";
    L01BF:
    Log("Add mutator " $ LeftOpt);
    MClass = class'Mutator'(Core.Object.DynamicLoadObject(LeftOpt, Core.Class));
    BaseMutator.AddMutator(Spawn(MClass));
    goto L0152;
    L020B:
    InOpt = ParseOption(Options, "GamePassword");
    if (InOpt == "") goto L0258;
    GamePassword = InOpt;
```

## Engine.GameInfo.ProcessServerTravel

```unrealscript
function void ProcessServerTravel(URL: string, bItems: bool)
{
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L0047;
    LocalLog.LogGameEnd("mapchange");
    LocalLog.StopLog();
    LocalLog.Destroy();
    LocalLog = None;
    L0047:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L008E;
    WorldLog.LogGameEnd("mapchange");
    WorldLog.StopLog();
    WorldLog.Destroy();
    WorldLog = None;
    L008E:
    Log("ProcessServerTravel:" @ URL);
    foreach (AllActors(PlayerPawn, P))  // end L0110
    if (class'Engine.NetConnection'(P.Player) == None) goto L00F5;
    P.ClientTravel(URL, 2, bItems);
    goto L010F;
    L00F5:
    LocalPlayer = P;
    P.PreClientTravel();
    L010F:
    continue;
    L0110:
    if (!(Level.NetMode == 2 && LocalPlayer != None)) goto L01F9;
    Level.NextURL = Level.NextURL $ "?Skin=" $ LocalPlayer.PlayerPawn.GetDefaultURL("Skin") $ "?Face=" $ LocalPlayer.PlayerPawn.GetDefaultURL("Face") $ "?Team=" $ LocalPlayer.PlayerPawn.GetDefaultURL("Team") $ "?Name=" $ LocalPlayer.PlayerPawn.GetDefaultURL("Name") $ "?Class=" $ LocalPlayer.PlayerPawn.GetDefaultURL("Class");
    L01F9:
    if (!(Level.NetMode != 1 && Level.NetMode != 2)) goto L023D;
    Level.NextSwitchCountdown = 0.0;
    L023D:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void PreLogin(Options: string, Address: string, out Error: string, out FailCode: string)
{
    Error = "";
    InPassword = ParseOption(Options, "Password");
    if (!(Level.NetMode != 0 && AtCapacity(Options))) goto L0058;
    Error = MaxedOutMessage;
    goto L00DE;
    L0058:
    if (!(GamePassword != "" && Caps(InPassword) != Caps(GamePassword) && (AdminPassword == "" || Caps(InPassword) != Caps(AdminPassword)))) goto L00DE;
    if (InPassword != "") goto L00C4;
    Error = NeedPassword;
    FailCode = "NEEDPW";
    goto L00DE;
    L00C4:
    Error = WrongPassword;
    FailCode = "WRONGPW";
    L00DE:
    if (CheckIPPolicy(Address)) goto L00F9;
    Error = IPBanned;
    L00F9:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.CheckWaterJump

```unrealscript
function bool CheckWaterJump(out WallNormal: Vector)
{
    if (carriedDecoration == None) goto L000D;
    return false;
    L000D:
    checkpoint = Rotation;
    checkpoint.Z = 0.0;
    checkNorm = Normal(checkpoint);
    checkpoint = Location + CollisionRadius * checkNorm;
    Extent = CollisionRadius * vect(1.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    Extent.Z = CollisionHeight;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, checkpoint, Location, true, Extent);
    if (!(HitActor != None && class'Pawn'(HitActor) == None)) goto L0138;
    WallNormal = -1 * HitNormal;
    Start = Location;
    Start.Z += 1.1 * MaxStepHeight;
    checkpoint = Start + 2 * CollisionRadius * checkNorm;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, checkpoint, Start, true);
    if (HitActor != None) goto L0138;
    return true;
    L0138:
    return false;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.CheckIPPolicy

```unrealscript
function bool CheckIPPolicy(Address: string)
{
    j = InStr(Address, ":");
    if (j == -1) goto L0031;
    Address = Left(Address, j);
    L0031:
    bAcceptAddress = true;
    i = 0;
    L0040:
    if (!(i < 50 && IPPolicies[i] != "")) goto L017E;
    j = InStr(IPPolicies[i], ",");
    if (j != -1) goto L0088;
    goto L0174;
    L0088:
    Policy = Left(IPPolicies[i], j);
    Mask = Mid(IPPolicies[i], j + 1);
    if (Policy == "ACCEPT") goto L00D8;
    bAcceptPolicy = true;
    goto L00F6;
    L00D8:
    if (Policy == "DENY") goto L00F3;
    bAcceptPolicy = false;
    goto L00F6;
    L00F3:
    goto L0174;
    L00F6:
    j = InStr(Mask, "*");
    if (j == -1) goto L014D;
    if (Left(Mask, j) != Left(Address, j)) goto L014A;
    bAcceptAddress = bAcceptPolicy;
    LastMatchingPolicy = i;
    L014A:
    goto L0174;
    L014D:
    if (Mask != Address) goto L0174;
    bAcceptAddress = bAcceptPolicy;
    LastMatchingPolicy = i;
    L0174:
    i++;
    goto L0040;
    L017E:
    if (bAcceptAddress) goto L01CB;
    Log("Denied connection for " $ Address $ " with IP policy " $ IPPolicies[LastMatchingPolicy]);
    L01CB:
    return bAcceptAddress;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.PainTimer

```unrealscript
function void PainTimer()
{
    if (!(Health < 0 || Level.NetMode == 3)) goto L0026;
    return;
    L0026:
    if (!(FootRegion.Zone.bPainZone)) goto L0181;
    depth = 0.4;
    if (!(Region.Zone.bPainZone)) goto L006B;
    depth += 0.4;
    L006B:
    if (!(HeadRegion.Zone.bPainZone)) goto L008E;
    depth += 0.2;
    L008E:
    if (FootRegion.Zone.DamagePerSec <= 0) goto L0128;
    if (!(IsA('PlayerPawn'))) goto L00DD;
    Level.Game.SpecialDamageString = FootRegion.Zone.DamageString;
    L00DD:
    TakeDamage(FootRegion.Zone.DamagePerSec * depth, None, Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), FootRegion.Zone.DamageType);
    goto L0168;
    L0128:
    if (Health >= default.Health) goto L0168;
    Health = Min(default.Health, Health - depth * FootRegion.Zone.DamagePerSec);
    L0168:
    if (Health <= 0) goto L017E;
    PainTime = 1.0;
    L017E:
    goto L01CE;
    L0181:
    if (!(HeadRegion.Zone.bWaterZone)) goto L01CE;
    TakeDamage(5, None, Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 'Drowned');
    if (Health <= 0) goto L01CE;
    PainTime = 2.0;
    L01CE:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.Login

```unrealscript
function PlayerPawn Login(Portal: string, Options: string, out Error: string, SpawnClass: class<Class>)
{
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0095;
    if (!(ClassIsChildOf(SpawnClass, Spectator))) goto L006C;
    if (!(NumSpectators >= MaxSpectators && (Level.NetMode != 2 || NumPlayers > 0))) goto L0069;
    Error = MaxedOutMessage;
    return None;
    L0069:
    goto L0095;
    L006C:
    if (!(MaxPlayers > 0 && NumPlayers >= MaxPlayers)) goto L0095;
    Error = MaxedOutMessage;
    return None;
    L0095:
    InName = Left(ParseOption(Options, "Name"), 20);
    InTeam = GetIntOption(Options, "Team", 0);
    InPassword = ParseOption(Options, "Password");
    InSkin = ParseOption(Options, "Skin");
    InFace = ParseOption(Options, "Face");
    InChecksum = ParseOption(Options, "Checksum");
    Log("Login:" @ InName);
    if (InPassword == "") goto L015D;
    Log("Password" @ InPassword);
    L015D:
    StartSpot = FindPlayerStart(None, InTeam, Portal);
    if (StartSpot != None) goto L018C;
    Error = FailedPlaceMessage;
    return None;
    L018C:
    PawnLink = Level.PawnList;
    L01A0:
    if (PawnLink == None) goto L02AB;
    TestPlayer = class'PlayerPawn'(PawnLink);
    if (!(TestPlayer != None && TestPlayer.Player == None && TestPlayer.PlayerReplicationInfo != None && TestPlayer.bIsPlayer && TestPlayer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName != PlayerReplicationInfo.default.PlayerName)) goto L0294;
    if (!(Level.NetMode == 0 || TestPlayer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName != InName && TestPlayer.Password != InPassword)) goto L0294;
    NewPlayer = TestPlayer;
    goto L02AB;
    L0294:
    PawnLink = PawnLink.nextPawn;
    goto L01A0;
    L02AB:
    if (NewPlayer != None) goto L035C;
    if (!((bHumansOnly || Level.bHumansOnly) && !SpawnClass.default.bIsHuman && !ClassIsChildOf(SpawnClass, Spectator))) goto L0308;
    SpawnClass = DefaultPlayerClass;
    L0308:
    NewPlayer = Spawn(SpawnClass, None, None, StartSpot.Location, StartSpot.Rotation);
    if (NewPlayer == None) goto L035C;
    NewPlayer.ViewRotation = StartSpot.Rotation;
    L035C:
    if (NewPlayer != None) goto L0399;
    Log("Couldn't spawn player at " $ StartSpot);
    Error = FailedSpawnMessage;
    return None;
    L0399:
    NewPlayer.SetMultiSkin(NewPlayer, InSkin, InFace, InTeam);
    NewPlayer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID = CurrentID++;
    NewPlayer.ClientSetRotation(NewPlayer.Rotation);
    if (InName != "") goto L040F;
    InName = DefaultPlayerName;
    L040F:
    if (!(Level.NetMode != 0 || NewPlayer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName == DefaultPlayerName)) goto L045A;
    ChangeName(NewPlayer, InName, false);
    L045A:
    if (ChangeTeam(NewPlayer, InTeam)) goto L047D;
    Error = FailedTeamMessage;
    return None;
    L047D:
    if (!(NewPlayer.IsA('Spectator') && Level.NetMode == 1)) goto L04B1;
    NumSpectators++;
    L04B1:
    NewPlayer.Password = InPassword;
    NewPlayer.bAdmin = AdminPassword != "" && Caps(InPassword) == Caps(AdminPassword);
    if (!(NewPlayer.bAdmin)) goto L0521;
    Log("Administrator logged in!");
    L0521:
    NewPlayer.GameReplicationInfo = GameReplicationInfo;
    if (!(Level.NetMode == 1 || Level.NetMode == 2)) goto L058A;
    BroadcastMessage(NewPlayer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName $ EnteredMessage, false);
    L058A:
    StartSpot.PlayTeleportEffect(NewPlayer, true);
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L05BE;
    LocalLog.LogPlayerConnect(NewPlayer);
    L05BE:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L05E2;
    WorldLog.LogPlayerConnect(NewPlayer, InChecksum);
    L05E2:
    if (NewPlayer.IsA('Spectator')) goto L05FF;
    NumPlayers++;
    L05FF:
    return NewPlayer;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void PostLogin(NewPlayer: PlayerPawn)
{
    NewPlayer.ClientSetMusic(Level.Song, Level.SongSection, Level.CdTrack, 3);
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0202;
    P = Level.PawnList;
    L0066:
    if (P == None) goto L0202;
    if (!(P.bIsPlayer && P != NewPlayer)) goto L01EB;
    if (!(P.bIsMultiSkinned)) goto L00FA;
    NewPlayer.ClientReplicateSkins(P.MultiSkins[0], P.MultiSkins[1], P.MultiSkins[2], P.MultiSkins[3]);
    goto L0117;
    L00FA:
    NewPlayer.ClientReplicateSkins(P.Skin);
    L0117:
    if (!(P.PlayerReplicationInfo != None && P.PlayerReplicationInfo.bWaitingPlayer && P.IsA('PlayerPawn'))) goto L01EB;
    if (!(NewPlayer.bIsMultiSkinned)) goto L01C9;
    class'PlayerPawn'(P).ClientReplicateSkins(NewPlayer.MultiSkins[0], NewPlayer.MultiSkins[1], NewPlayer.MultiSkins[2], NewPlayer.MultiSkins[3]);
    goto L01EB;
    L01C9:
    class'PlayerPawn'(P).ClientReplicateSkins(NewPlayer.Skin);
    L01EB:
    P = P.nextPawn;
    goto L0066;
    L0202:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.Killed

```unrealscript
function void Killed(Killer: Pawn, Other: Pawn, DamageType: name)
{
    if (!(Other.bIsPlayer)) goto L05EE;
    if (!(Killer != None && !Killer.bIsPlayer)) goto L00DF;
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0093;
    Message = Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName $ Killer.KillMessage(DamageType, Other);
    BroadcastMessage(Message, false, 'DeathMessage');
    L0093:
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L00B8;
    LocalLog.LogSuicide(Other, DamageType, None);
    L00B8:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L00DD;
    WorldLog.LogSuicide(Other, DamageType, None);
    L00DD:
    return;
    L00DF:
    if (!(DamageType == 'SpecialDamage' && SpecialDamageString != "")) goto L0177;
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L016F;
    BroadcastMessage(ParseKillMessage(Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName, Killer.Weapon.ItemName, SpecialDamageString), false, 'DeathMessage');
    L016F:
    bSpecialDamage = true;
    L0177:
    if (!(Killer == Other || Killer == None)) goto L0386;
    if (DamageType != 'None') goto L01F7;
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L01CB;
    LocalLog.LogSuicide(Other, 'Unknown', Killer);
    L01CB:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L01F4;
    WorldLog.LogSuicide(Other, 'Unknown', Killer);
    L01F4:
    goto L0249;
    L01F7:
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L0220;
    LocalLog.LogSuicide(Other, DamageType, Killer);
    L0220:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L0249;
    WorldLog.LogSuicide(Other, DamageType, Killer);
    L0249:
    if (bSpecialDamage) goto L0383;
    if (DamageType != 'Fell') goto L0282;
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 2, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    goto L0383;
    L0282:
    if (DamageType != 'Eradicated') goto L02B0;
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 3, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    goto L0383;
    L02B0:
    if (DamageType != 'Drowned') goto L02DE;
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 4, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    goto L0383;
    L02DE:
    if (DamageType != 'Burned') goto L030C;
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 5, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    goto L0383;
    L030C:
    if (DamageType != 'Corroded') goto L033A;
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 6, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    goto L0383;
    L033A:
    if (DamageType != 'Mortared') goto L0368;
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 7, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    goto L0383;
    L0368:
    BroadcastLocalizedMessage(DeathMessageClass, 1, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo, None);
    L0383:
    goto L05EE;
    L0386:
    AddEqual_FloatFloat(Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.Deaths, 1);
    if (!(Killer.bIsPlayer)) goto L05EE;
    KillerWeapon = "None";
    if (Killer.Weapon == None) goto L03F0;
    KillerWeapon = Killer.Weapon.ItemName;
    L03F0:
    OtherWeapon = "None";
    if (Other.Weapon == None) goto L042D;
    OtherWeapon = Other.Weapon.ItemName;
    L042D:
    if (Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.Team != Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.Team) goto L0513;
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L04B9;
    LocalLog.LogTeamKill(Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, KillerWeapon, OtherWeapon, DamageType);
    L04B9:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L0510;
    WorldLog.LogTeamKill(Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, KillerWeapon, OtherWeapon, DamageType);
    L0510:
    goto L05C1;
    L0513:
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L056A;
    LocalLog.LogKill(Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, KillerWeapon, OtherWeapon, DamageType);
    L056A:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L05C1;
    WorldLog.LogKill(Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerID, KillerWeapon, OtherWeapon, DamageType);
    L05C1:
    if (!(!bSpecialDamage && Other != None)) goto L05EE;
    BroadcastRegularDeathMessage(Killer, Other, DamageType);
    L05EE:
    ScoreKill(Killer, Other);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.FeigningDeath.Rise


## Engine.PlayerReplicationInfo.Timer

```unrealscript
function void Timer()

    PlayerLocation = None;
    if (PlayerZone == None) goto L00B8;
    L = PlayerZone.locationid;
    L0031:
    if (L == None) goto L00B8;
    dist = VSize(Owner.Location - L.Location);

    PlayerLocation = L;
    minDist = dist;
    L00A1:
    L = L.NextLocation;

    L00B8:

    L00C6:

}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.DiscardInventory

```unrealscript
function void DiscardInventory(Other: Pawn)
{
    if (Other.DropWhenKilled == None) goto L00FD;
    dropped = Spawn(Other.DropWhenKilled, None, None, Other.Location);
    Inv = class'Inventory'(dropped);
    if (Inv == None) goto L0079;
    Inv.RespawnTime = 0.0;
    Inv.BecomePickup();
    L0079:
    if (dropped == None) goto L00DD;
    dropped.RemoteRole = 1;
    dropped.SetPhysics(2);
    dropped.bCollideWorld = true;
    dropped.Velocity = Other.Velocity + VRand() * 280;
    L00DD:
    if (Inv == None) goto L00FD;
    Inv.GotoState('Pickup', 'dropped');
    L00FD:
    if (!(Other.Weapon != None && Other.Weapon.Class != Level.Game.BaseMutator.MutatedDefaultWeapon() && Other.Weapon.bCanThrow)) goto L0264;
    speed = VSize(Other.Velocity);
    weap = Other.Weapon;
    if (speed == 0) goto L01E6;
    weap.Velocity = Normal(Other.Velocity / speed + 0.5 * VRand()) * (speed + 280);
    goto L0231;
    L01E6:
    weap.Velocity.X = 0.0;
    weap.Velocity.Y = 0.0;
    weap.Velocity.Z = 0.0;
    L0231:
    Other.TossWeapon();
    if (weap.PickupAmmoCount != 0) goto L0264;
    weap.PickupAmmoCount = 1;
    L0264:
    Other.Weapon = None;
    Other.SelectedItem = None;
    Inv = Other.Inventory;
    L0298:
    if (Inv == None) goto L02C6;
    Inv.Destroy();
    Inv = Inv.Inventory;
    goto L0298;
    L02C6:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.EndGame

```unrealscript
function void EndGame(Reason: string)
{
    if (SetEndCams(Reason)) goto L001A;
    bOverTime = true;
    return;
    L001A:
    bGameEnded = true;
    foreach (AllActors(Actor, A, 'EndGame'))  // end L0049
    A.Trigger(self, None);
    continue;
    L0049:
    if (LocalLog == None) goto L00A3;
    LocalLog.LogGameEnd(Reason);
    LocalLog.StopLog();
    if (bBatchLocal) goto L0090;
    LocalLog.StatLog.ExecuteSilentLogBatcher();
    L0090:
    LocalLog.Destroy();
    LocalLog = None;
    L00A3:
    if (WorldLog == None) goto L00F3;
    WorldLog.LogGameEnd(Reason);
    WorldLog.StopLog();
    WorldLog.StatLog.ExecuteWorldLogBatcher();
    WorldLog.Destroy();
    WorldLog = None;
    L00F3:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.SetEndCams

```unrealscript
function bool SetEndCams(Reason: string)
{
    aPawn = Level.PawnList;
    L0014:
    if (aPawn == None) goto L0067;
    if (!(aPawn.bIsPlayer)) goto L0050;
    aPawn.GotoState('GameEnded');
    aPawn.ClientGameEnded();
    L0050:
    aPawn = aPawn.nextPawn;
    goto L0014;
    L0067:
    return true;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerWalking.Dodge

```unrealscript
function void Dodge(DodgeMove: EDodgeDir)
{
    if (!(bIsCrouching || Physics != 1)) goto L001B;
    return;
    L001B:
    GetAxes(Rotation, X, Y, Z);
    if (DodgeMove != 3) goto L0070;
    Velocity = 1.5 * GroundSpeed * X + Dot_VectorVector(Velocity, Y) * Y;
    goto L012A;
    L0070:
    if (DodgeMove != 4) goto L00AF;
    Velocity = -1.5 * GroundSpeed * X + Dot_VectorVector(Velocity, Y) * Y;
    goto L012A;
    L00AF:
    if (DodgeMove != 1) goto L00EE;
    Velocity = 1.5 * GroundSpeed * Y + Dot_VectorVector(Velocity, X) * X;
    goto L012A;
    L00EE:
    if (DodgeMove != 2) goto L012A;
    Velocity = -1.5 * GroundSpeed * Y + Dot_VectorVector(Velocity, X) * X;
    L012A:
    Velocity.Z = 160.0;
    Actor.PlayOwnedSound(JumpSound, 5, 1.0, true, 800.0, 1.0);
    PlayDodge(DodgeMove);
    DodgeDir = 5;
    SetPhysics(2);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Mutator.ReplaceWith

```unrealscript
function bool ReplaceWith(Other: Actor, aClassName: string)
{
    if (!(Other.IsA('Inventory') && Other.Location == vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0))) goto L0038;
    return false;
    L0038:
    aClass = class'Actor'(Core.Object.DynamicLoadObject(aClassName, Core.Class));
    if (aClass == None) goto L0097;
    A = Spawn(aClass, None, Other.Tag, Other.Location, Other.Rotation);
    L0097:
    if (!(Other.IsA('Inventory'))) goto L01C6;
    if (class'Inventory'(Other).myMarker == None) goto L0183;
    class'Inventory'(Other).myMarker.markedItem = class'Inventory'(A);
    if (class'Inventory'(A) == None) goto L016B;
    class'Inventory'(A).myMarker = class'Inventory'(Other).myMarker;
    A.SetLocation(A.Location + (A.CollisionHeight - Other.CollisionHeight) * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
    L016B:
    class'Inventory'(Other).myMarker = None;
    goto L01C6;
    L0183:
    if (!(A.IsA('Inventory'))) goto L01C6;
    class'Inventory'(A).bHeldItem = true;
    class'Inventory'(A).RespawnTime = 0.0;
    L01C6:
    if (A == None) goto L020D;
    A.Event = Other.Event;
    A.Tag = Other.Tag;
    return true;
    L020D:
    return false;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.StatLog.GetAbsoluteTime

```unrealscript
function string GetAbsoluteTime()
{

    if (Level.Month >= 10) goto L004F;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Month;
    goto L0070;
    L004F:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Month;
    L0070:
    if (Level.Day >= 10) goto L00AA;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Day;
    goto L00CB;
    L00AA:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Day;
    L00CB:
    if (Level.Hour >= 10) goto L0105;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Hour;
    goto L0126;
    L0105:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Hour;
    L0126:
    if (Level.Minute >= 10) goto L0160;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Minute;
    goto L0181;
    L0160:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Minute;
    L0181:
    if (Level.Second >= 10) goto L01BB;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Second;
    goto L01DC;
    L01BB:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Second;
    L01DC:
    if (Level.Millisecond >= 10) goto L0216;
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ ".0" $ Level.Millisecond;
    goto L0237;
    L0216:
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ Level.Millisecond;
    L0237:
    GMTRef = StatLog.GetGMTRef();
    AbsoluteTime = AbsoluteTime $ "." $ GMTRef;
    TimeStamp = 0.0;
    return AbsoluteTime;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerWalking.AnimEnd

```unrealscript
function void AnimEnd()
{
    bAnimTransition = false;
    if (Physics != 1) goto L01B7;
    if (bIsCrouching) goto L0077;
    if (!(!bIsTurning && Velocity.X * Velocity.X + Velocity.Y * Velocity.Y < 1000)) goto L006E;
    PlayDuck();
    goto L0074;
    L006E:
    PlayCrawling();
    L0074:
    goto L01B4;
    L0077:
    MyAnimGroup = GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence);
    if (Velocity.X * Velocity.X + Velocity.Y * Velocity.Y >= 1000) goto L00EC;
    if (MyAnimGroup != 'Waiting') goto L00D6;
    PlayWaiting();
    goto L00E9;
    L00D6:
    bAnimTransition = true;
    TweenToWaiting(0.2);
    L00E9:
    goto L01B4;
    L00EC:
    if (bIsWalking) goto L0156;
    if (!(MyAnimGroup == 'Waiting' || MyAnimGroup == 'Landing' || MyAnimGroup == 'Gesture' || MyAnimGroup == 'TakeHit')) goto L014D;
    TweenToWalking(0.1);
    bAnimTransition = true;
    goto L0153;
    L014D:
    PlayWalking();
    L0153:
    goto L01B4;
    L0156:
    if (!(MyAnimGroup == 'Waiting' || MyAnimGroup == 'Landing' || MyAnimGroup == 'Gesture' || MyAnimGroup == 'TakeHit')) goto L01AE;
    bAnimTransition = true;
    TweenToRunning(0.1);
    goto L01B4;
    L01AE:
    PlayRunning();
    L01B4:
    goto L01BD;
    L01B7:
    PlayInAir();
    L01BD:
    return;
}
```
