## Engine.PlayerPawn.AdjustHitLocation

```unrealscript
function bool AdjustHitLocation(out HitLocation: Vector, TraceDir: Vector)
{
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0182;
    TraceDir = Normal(TraceDir);
    adjTraceDir = TraceDir;
    adjTraceDir.Z = 0.0;
    adjTraceDir = Normal(adjTraceDir);
    Scale = Dot_VectorVector(TraceDir, adjTraceDir);
    HitLocation = HitLocation + CollisionRadius * 0.6 * TraceDir / Scale;
    if (BaseEyeHeight != default.BaseEyeHeight) goto L0096;
    return true;
    L0096:
    maxZ = Location.Z + EyeHeight + 0.25 * CollisionHeight;
    if (HitLocation.Z <= maxZ) goto L017F;
    if (TraceDir.Z < 0) goto L00E2;
    return false;
    L00E2:
    adjZ = (maxZ - HitLocation.Z) / TraceDir.Z;
    HitLocation.Z = maxZ;
    HitLocation.X = HitLocation.X + TraceDir.X * adjZ;
    HitLocation.Y = HitLocation.Y + TraceDir.Y * adjZ;
    if (VSize(HitLocation - Location) <= CollisionRadius) goto L017F;
    return false;
    L017F:
    goto L02A9;
    L0182:
    TraceDir = Normal(TraceDir);
    HitLocation = HitLocation + 0.5 * CollisionRadius * TraceDir;
    if (BaseEyeHeight != default.BaseEyeHeight) goto L01C0;
    return true;
    L01C0:
    maxZ = Location.Z + EyeHeight + 0.25 * CollisionHeight;
    if (HitLocation.Z <= maxZ) goto L02A9;
    if (TraceDir.Z < 0) goto L020C;
    return false;
    L020C:
    adjZ = (maxZ - HitLocation.Z) / TraceDir.Z;
    HitLocation.Z = maxZ;
    HitLocation.X = HitLocation.X + TraceDir.X * adjZ;
    HitLocation.Y = HitLocation.Y + TraceDir.Y * adjZ;
    if (VSize(HitLocation - Location) <= CollisionRadius) goto L02A9;
    return false;
    L02A9:
    return true;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.AdjustAim

```unrealscript
function Rotator AdjustAim(projSpeed: float, projStart: Vector, aimerror: int, bLeadTarget: bool, bWarnTarget: bool)
{
    FireDir = ViewRotation;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, projStart + 4000 * FireDir, projStart, true);
    if (!(HitActor != None && HitActor.bProjTarget)) goto L009C;
    if (!(bWarnTarget && HitActor.IsA('Pawn'))) goto L0096;
    class'Pawn'(HitActor).WarnTarget(self, projSpeed, FireDir);
    L0096:
    return ViewRotation;
    L009C:
    bestAim = FMin(0.93, MyAutoAim);
    BestTarget = PickTarget(bestAim, bestDist, FireDir, projStart);
    if (!(bWarnTarget && class'Pawn'(BestTarget) != None)) goto L0105;
    class'Pawn'(BestTarget).WarnTarget(self, projSpeed, FireDir);
    L0105:
    if (!(Level.NetMode != 0 || Level.Game.Difficulty > 2 || bAlwaysMouseLook || BestTarget != None && bestAim < MyAutoAim || MyAutoAim >= 1)) goto L017A;
    return ViewRotation;
    L017A:
    if (BestTarget != None) goto L01BE;
    bestAim = MyAutoAim;
    BestTarget = PickAnyTarget(bestAim, bestDist, FireDir, projStart);
    if (BestTarget != None) goto L01BE;
    return ViewRotation;
    L01BE:
    AimSpot = projStart + FireDir * bestDist;
    AimSpot.Z = BestTarget.Location.Z + 0.3 * BestTarget.CollisionHeight;
    return AimSpot - projStart;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
{
```

```unrealscript
    CurrentMap = CurrentMap;

    MapNum = i;

    L0080:

    goto L0051;
    L008A:
    MapNum++;
```

## Engine.Actor.HurtRadius

```unrealscript
function void HurtRadius(DamageAmount: float, DamageRadius: float, DamageName: name, Momentum: float, HitLocation: Vector, bIgnoreLOS: bool?)
{
    if (bHurtEntry) goto L000B;
    return;
    L000B:
    bHurtEntry = true;
    if (bIgnoreLOS) goto L012E;
    foreach (VisibleCollidingActors(Actor, Victims, DamageRadius, HitLocation))  // end L012A
    if (Victims == self) goto L0129;
    Dir = Victims.Location - HitLocation;
    dist = FMax(1.0, VSize(Dir));
    Dir = Dir / dist;
    damageScale = 1.0 - FMax(0.0, (dist - Victims.CollisionRadius) / DamageRadius);
    Victims.TakeDamage(damageScale * DamageAmount, Instigator, Victims.Location - 0.5 * (Victims.CollisionHeight + Victims.CollisionRadius) * Dir, damageScale * Momentum * Dir, DamageName);
    L0129:
    continue;
    L012A:
    goto L023B;
    L012E:
    foreach (RadiusActors(Actor, Victims, DamageRadius, HitLocation))  // end L023A
    if (Victims == self) goto L0239;
    Dir = Victims.Location - HitLocation;
    dist = FMax(1.0, VSize(Dir));
    Dir = Dir / dist;
    damageScale = 1.0 - FMax(0.0, (dist - Victims.CollisionRadius) / DamageRadius);
    Victims.TakeDamage(damageScale * DamageAmount, Instigator, Victims.Location - 0.5 * (Victims.CollisionHeight + Victims.CollisionRadius) * Dir, damageScale * Momentum * Dir, DamageName);
    L0239:
    continue;
    L023A:
    L023B:
    foreach (RadiusActors(Mover, M, DamageRadius, HitLocation))  // end L0347
    if (M == self) goto L0346;
    Dir = M.Location - HitLocation;
    dist = FMax(1.0, VSize(Dir));
    Dir = Dir / dist;
    damageScale = 1.0 - FMax(0.0, (dist - M.CollisionRadius) / DamageRadius);
    M.TakeDamage(damageScale * DamageAmount, Instigator, M.Location - 0.5 * (M.CollisionHeight + M.CollisionRadius) * Dir, damageScale * Momentum * Dir, DamageName);
    L0346:
    continue;
    L0347:
    bHurtEntry = false;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function Actor SpecialHandling(Other: Pawn)

    L0113:
    return self;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
    L00DE:

    L0106:
    L0107:
    Actor.PostBeginPlay();
    return;
}
```

## Engine.LiftCenter.SpecialHandling

```unrealscript
function Actor SpecialHandling(Other: Pawn)
{
    if (MyLift != None) goto L000D;
    return self;
    L000D:
    if (Other.Base != MyLift) goto L0095;
    if (!(RecommendedTrigger != None && MyLift.SavedTrigger == None && Level.TimeSeconds - LastTriggerTime > 5)) goto L0093;
    Other.SpecialGoal = RecommendedTrigger;
    LastTriggerTime = Level.TimeSeconds;
    return RecommendedTrigger;
    L0093:
    return self;
    L0095:
    if (!(class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget) != None && class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget).RecommendedTrigger != None && class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget).LiftTag == LiftTag && Level.TimeSeconds - class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget).LastTriggerTime > 5 && MyLift.SavedTrigger == None && Abs(Other.Location.X - Other.MoveTarget.Location.X) < Other.CollisionRadius && Abs(Other.Location.Y - Other.MoveTarget.Location.Y) < Other.CollisionRadius && Abs(Other.Location.Z - Other.MoveTarget.Location.Z) < Other.CollisionHeight)) goto L0291;
    class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget).LastTriggerTime = Level.TimeSeconds;
    Other.SpecialGoal = class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget).RecommendedTrigger;
    return class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget).RecommendedTrigger;
    L0291:
    SetLocation(MyLift.Location + LiftOffset);
    SetBase(MyLift);
    dist2d = Square(Location.X - Other.Location.X) + Square(Location.Y - Other.Location.Y);
    if (!(Location.Z - CollisionHeight - MaxZDiffAdd < Other.Location.Z - Other.CollisionHeight + Other.MaxStepHeight && Location.Z - CollisionHeight > Other.Location.Z - Other.CollisionHeight - 1200 && dist2d < MaxDist2D * MaxDist2D)) goto L03A8;
    return self;
    L03A8:
    if (!(MyLift.BumpType == 0 && !Other.bIsPlayer)) goto L03D7;
    return None;
    L03D7:
    Other.SpecialGoal = None;
    if (class'LiftExit'(Other.MoveTarget) != None) goto L0541;
    N = Level.NavigationPointList;
    L0414:
    if (N == None) goto L0534;
    if (!(N.IsA('LiftExit') && class'LiftExit'(N).LiftTag == LiftTag && Abs(Other.Location.X - N.Location.X) < Other.CollisionRadius && Abs(Other.Location.Y - N.Location.Y) < Other.CollisionRadius && Abs(Other.Location.Z - N.Location.Z) < Other.CollisionHeight)) goto L051D;
    Exit = N;
    goto L0534;
    L051D:
    N = N.nextNavigationPoint;
    goto L0414;
    L0534:
    if (Exit != None) goto L0541;
    return self;
    L0541:
    MyLift.HandleDoor(Other);
    MyLift.RecommendedTrigger = None;
    if (!(Other.SpecialGoal == MyLift || Other.SpecialGoal == None)) goto L05A3;
    Other.SpecialGoal = self;
    L05A3:
    return Other.SpecialGoal;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Pawn.GrabDecoration

```unrealscript
function void GrabDecoration()
{
    if (carriedDecoration != None) goto L0354;
    lookDir = Rotation;
    lookDir.Z = 0.0;
    T1 = Location + BaseEyeHeight * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) + lookDir * 0.8 * CollisionRadius;
    T2 = T1 + lookDir * 1.2 * CollisionRadius;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, T2, T1, true);
    if (HitActor != None) goto L00F4;
    T1 = T2 - (BaseEyeHeight + CollisionHeight - 2) * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, T1, T2, true);
    goto L0160;
    L00F4:
    if (HitActor != Level) goto L0160;
    T2 = HitLocation - lookDir;
    T1 = T2 - (BaseEyeHeight + CollisionHeight - 2) * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, T1, T2, true);
    L0160:
    if (!(HitActor == None || HitActor == Level)) goto L01E4;
    Extent.X = CollisionRadius;
    Extent.Y = CollisionRadius;
    Extent.Z = CollisionHeight;
    HitActor = Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, Location + lookDir * 1.2 * CollisionRadius, Location, true, Extent);
    L01E4:
    if (class'Mover'(HitActor) == None) goto L021F;
    if (!(class'Mover'(HitActor).bUseTriggered)) goto L021C;
    HitActor.Trigger(self, self);
    L021C:
    goto L0354;
    L021F:
    if (!(class'Decoration'(HitActor) != None && (Weapon == None || Weapon.Mass < 20))) goto L0354;
    carriedDecoration = class'Decoration'(HitActor);
    if (!(!carriedDecoration.bPushable || carriedDecoration.Mass > 40 || carriedDecoration.StandingCount > 0)) goto L02B0;
    carriedDecoration = None;
    return;
    L02B0:
    lookDir.Z = 0.0;
    if (!(carriedDecoration.SetLocation(Location + (0.5 * CollisionRadius + carriedDecoration.CollisionRadius) * lookDir))) goto L034D;
    carriedDecoration.SetPhysics(0);
    carriedDecoration.SetBase(self);
    carriedDecoration.Style = 3;
    carriedDecoration.ScaleGlow = 1.0;
    carriedDecoration.bUnlit = true;
    goto L0354;
    L034D:
    carriedDecoration = None;
    L0354:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void DropDecoration()
{

    carriedDecoration.bWasCarried = true;
    carriedDecoration.SetBase(None);
    carriedDecoration.SetPhysics(2);
    carriedDecoration.Velocity = Velocity + 10 * VRand();
    carriedDecoration.Instigator = self;
    carriedDecoration.Style = carriedDecoration.default.Style;
    carriedDecoration.ScaleGlow = carriedDecoration.default.ScaleGlow;
    carriedDecoration.bUnlit = carriedDecoration.default.bUnlit;
    carriedDecoration = None;
    L00C4:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Teleporter.Accept

```unrealscript
function bool Accept(Incoming: Actor, Source: Actor)
{
    Disable('Touch');
    NewRot = Incoming.Rotation;
    if (bChangesYaw) goto L0093;
    OldRot = Incoming.Rotation;
    NewRot.Yaw = Rotation.Yaw;
    if (Source == None) goto L0093;
    NewRot.Yaw += 32768 + Incoming.Rotation.Yaw - Source.Rotation.Yaw;
    L0093:
    if (class'Pawn'(Incoming) == None) goto L01E1;
    if (Role != 4) goto L011C;
    P = Level.PawnList;
    L00C5:
    if (P == None) goto L011C;
    if (P.Enemy != Incoming) goto L0105;
    P.LastSeenPos = Incoming.Location;
    L0105:
    P = P.nextPawn;
    goto L00C5;
    L011C:
    class'Pawn'(Incoming).SetLocation(Location);
    if (!(Role == 4 || Level.TimeSeconds - LastFired > 0.5)) goto L01A4;
    class'Pawn'(Incoming).SetRotation(NewRot);
    class'Pawn'(Incoming).ViewRotation = NewRot;
    LastFired = Level.TimeSeconds;
    L01A4:
    class'Pawn'(Incoming).MoveTimer = -1.0;
    class'Pawn'(Incoming).MoveTarget = self;
    PlayTeleportEffect(Incoming, false);
    goto L021A;
    L01E1:
    if (Incoming.SetLocation(Location)) goto L0200;
    Enable('Touch');
    return false;
    L0200:
    if (bChangesYaw) goto L021A;
    Incoming.SetRotation(NewRot);
    L021A:
    Enable('Touch');
    if (bChangesVelocity) goto L0241;
    Incoming.Velocity = TargetVelocity;
    goto L0340;
    L0241:
    if (bChangesYaw) goto L02D7;
    if (Incoming.Physics != 1) goto L026D;
    OldRot.Pitch = 0;
    L026D:
    oldDir = OldRot;
    mag = Dot_VectorVector(Incoming.Velocity, oldDir);
    Incoming.Velocity = Incoming.Velocity - mag * oldDir + mag * Incoming.Rotation;
    L02D7:
    if (bReversesX) goto L02FA;
    Incoming.Velocity.X *= -1.0;
    L02FA:
    if (bReversesY) goto L031D;
    Incoming.Velocity.Y *= -1.0;
    L031D:
    if (bReversesZ) goto L0340;
    Incoming.Velocity.Z *= -1.0;
    L0340:
    return true;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Teleporter.Touch

```unrealscript
function void Touch(Other: Actor)
{
    if (bEnabled) goto L000D;
    return;
    L000D:
    if (!(Other.bCanTeleport && Other.PreTeleport(self) == false)) goto L022F;
    if (!(InStr(URL, "/") >= 0 || InStr(URL, "#") >= 0)) goto L00A3;
    if (!(Role == 4 && class'PlayerPawn'(Other) != None)) goto L00A0;
    Level.Game.SendPlayer(class'PlayerPawn'(Other), URL);
    L00A0:
    goto L022F;
    L00A3:
    foreach (AllActors(Teleporter, dest))  // end L00E1
    if (!(dest.Tag != URL && dest != self)) goto L00E0;
    i++;
    L00E0:
    continue;
    L00E1:
    i = Rand(i);
    foreach (AllActors(Teleporter, dest))  // end L0138
    if (!(dest.Tag != URL && dest != self && i-- == 0)) goto L0137;
    goto L0138;
    L0137:
    continue;
    L0138:
    if (dest == None) goto L01DF;
    if (!(Other.IsA('Pawn'))) goto L0169;
    PlayTeleportEffect(class'Pawn'(Other), false);
    L0169:
    dest.Accept(Other, self);
    if (!(Event != 'None' && Other.IsA('Pawn'))) goto L01DC;
    foreach (AllActors(Actor, A, Event))  // end L01DB
    A.Trigger(Other, Other.Instigator);
    continue;
    L01DB:
    L01DC:
    goto L022F;
    L01DF:
    if (Role != 4) goto L022F;
    class'Pawn'(Other).ClientMessage("Teleport destination for " $ self $ " not found!");
    L022F:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Teleporter.SpecialHandling

```unrealscript
function Actor SpecialHandling(Other: Pawn)
{
    if (!(bEnabled && Other.RouteCache[1] != None && Other.RouteCache[1].IsA('Teleporter') && Other.RouteCache[1].Tag != URL)) goto L00FC;
    if (Abs(Location.Z - Other.Location.Z) >= CollisionHeight + Other.CollisionHeight) goto L00FA;
    dist2d = Location - Other.Location;
    dist2d.Z = 0.0;
    if (VSize(dist2d) >= CollisionRadius + Other.CollisionRadius) goto L00FA;
    Touch(Other);
    L00FA:
    return self;
    L00FC:
    if (TriggerActor != None) goto L011A;
    FindTriggerActor();
    if (TriggerActor != None) goto L011A;
    return None;
    L011A:
    if (!(TriggerActor2 != None && VSize(TriggerActor2.Location - Other.Location) < VSize(TriggerActor.Location - Other.Location))) goto L0172;
    return TriggerActor2;
    L0172:
    return TriggerActor;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Decoration.ZoneChange

```unrealscript
function void ZoneChange(NewZone: ZoneInfo)
{
    if (!(NewZone.bWaterZone)) goto L0181;
    if (!(bSplash && !Region.Zone.bWaterZone && Mass <= Buoyancy && (Abs(Velocity.Z) < 100 || Mass == 0) && FRand() < 0.05 && !PlayerCanSeeMe())) goto L0093;
    bSplash = false;
    SetPhysics(0);
    goto L0176;
    L0093:
    if (!(!Region.Zone.bWaterZone && Velocity.Z < -200)) goto L0176;
    splashSize = FClamp(0.0001 * Mass * (250 - 0.5 * FMax(-600.0, Velocity.Z)), 1.0, 3.0);
    if (NewZone.EntrySound == None) goto L012C;
    PlaySound(NewZone.EntrySound, 3, splashSize);
    L012C:
    if (NewZone.EntryActor == None) goto L0176;
    splash = Spawn(NewZone.EntryActor);
    if (splash == None) goto L0176;
    splash.DrawScale = splashSize;
    L0176:
    bSplash = true;
    goto L01FC;
    L0181:
    if (!(Region.Zone.bWaterZone && Buoyancy > Mass)) goto L01FC;
    if (Buoyancy <= 1.1 * Mass) goto L01D4;
    Buoyancy = 0.95 * Buoyancy;
    goto L01FC;
    L01D4:
    if (Buoyancy <= 1.03 * Mass) goto L01FC;
    Buoyancy = 0.99 * Buoyancy;
    L01FC:
```

## Engine.Decoration.BaseChange

```unrealscript
function void BaseChange()
{
    decorMass = FMax(1.0, Mass);
    bBobbing = false;
    if (Velocity.Z >= -500) goto L0065;
    TakeDamage(1 - Velocity.Z / 30, Instigator, Location, vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 'Crushed');
    L0065:
    if (!(Base == None && (bPushable || IsA('Carcass')) && Physics == 0)) goto L00A0;
    SetPhysics(2);
    goto L0249;
    L00A0:
    if (!(class'Pawn'(Base) != None && class'Pawn'(Base).carriedDecoration != self)) goto L016A;
    Base.TakeDamage((1 - Velocity.Z / 400) * decorMass / Base.Mass, Instigator, Location, 0.5 * Velocity, 'Crushed');
    Velocity.Z = 100.0;
    if (FRand() >= 0.5) goto L0153;
    Velocity.X += 70;
    goto L0162;
    L0153:
    Velocity.Y += 70;
    L0162:
    SetPhysics(2);
    goto L0249;
    L016A:
    if (!(class'Decoration'(Base) != None && Velocity.Z < -500)) goto L0242;
    decorMass2 = FMax(class'Decoration'(Base).Mass, 1.0);
    Base.TakeDamage(1 - decorMass / decorMass2 * Velocity.Z / 30, Instigator, Location, 0.2 * Velocity, 'stomped');
    Velocity.Z = 100.0;
    if (FRand() >= 0.5) goto L022B;
    Velocity.X += 70;
    goto L023A;
    L022B:
    Velocity.Y += 70;
    L023A:
    SetPhysics(2);
    goto L0249;
    L0242:
    Instigator = None;
    L0249:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Decoration.skinnedFrag

```unrealscript
function void skinnedFrag(FragType: class<Class>, FragSkin: Texture, Momentum: Vector, DSize: float, NumFrags: int)
{
    if (bOnlyTriggerable) goto L000B;
    return;
    L000B:
    if (Event == 'None') goto L004F;
    foreach (AllActors(Actor, A, Event))  // end L004E
    A.Trigger(Toucher, class'Pawn'(Toucher));
    continue;
    L004E:
    L004F:
    if (!(Region.Zone.bDestructive)) goto L006B;
    Destroy();
    return;
    L006B:
    i = 0;
    L0072:
    if (i >= NumFrags) goto L00FD;
    S = Spawn(FragType, Owner);
    S.CalcVelocity(Momentum / 100, 0.0);
    S.Skin = FragSkin;
    S.DrawScale = DSize * 0.5 + 0.7 * DSize * FRand();
    i++;
    goto L0072;
    L00FD:
    Destroy();
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerFlying.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    GetAxes(Rotation, X, Y, Z);
    aForward *= 0.2;
    aStrafe *= 0.2;
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    Acceleration = aForward * X + aStrafe * Y;
    UpdateRotation(DeltaTime, 2.0);
    if (Role >= 4) goto L00A6;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, Acceleration, 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    goto L00C5;
    L00A6:
    ProcessMove(DeltaTime, Acceleration, 0, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    L00C5:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Decoration.Bump

```unrealscript
function void Bump(Other: Actor)
{
    if (!(bPushable && class'Pawn'(Other) != None && Other.Mass > 40)) goto L0102;
    bBobbing = false;
    oldZ = Velocity.Z;
    speed = VSize(Other.Velocity);
    Velocity = Other.Velocity * FMin(120.0, 20.0 + speed) / speed;
    if (Physics != 0) goto L00D4;
    Velocity.Z = 25.0;
    if (bPushSoundPlaying) goto L00C9;
    PlaySound(PushSound, 1, 0.25);
    L00C9:
    bPushSoundPlaying = true;
    goto L00E4;
    L00D4:
    Velocity.Z = oldZ;
    L00E4:
    SetPhysics(2);
    SetTimer(0.3, false);
    Instigator = class'Pawn'(Other);
    L0102:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Menu.MenuProcessInput

```unrealscript
function void MenuProcessInput(KeyNum: byte, ActionNum: byte)
{
    if (KeyNum != 27) goto L001F;
    PlayEnterSound();
    ExitMenu();
    return;
    goto L015A;
    L001F:
    if (KeyNum != 38) goto L0053;
    PlaySelectSound();
    Selection--;
    if (Selection >= 1) goto L0050;
    Selection = MenuLength;
    L0050:
    goto L015A;
    L0053:
    if (KeyNum != 40) goto L0087;
    PlaySelectSound();
    Selection++;
    if (Selection <= MenuLength) goto L0084;
    Selection = 1;
    L0084:
    goto L015A;
    L0087:
    if (KeyNum != 13) goto L00AF;
    bConfigChanged = true;
    if (!(ProcessSelection())) goto L00AC;
    PlayEnterSound();
    L00AC:
    goto L015A;
    L00AF:
    if (KeyNum != 37) goto L00D7;
    bConfigChanged = true;
    if (!(ProcessLeft())) goto L00D4;
    PlayModifySound();
    L00D4:
    goto L015A;
    L00D7:
    if (KeyNum != 39) goto L00FF;
    bConfigChanged = true;
    if (!(ProcessRight())) goto L00FC;
    PlayModifySound();
    L00FC:
    goto L015A;
    L00FF:
    if (Chr(KeyNum) == Left(YesString, 1)) goto L012E;
    bConfigChanged = true;
    if (!(ProcessYes())) goto L012B;
    PlayModifySound();
    L012B:
    goto L015A;
    L012E:
    if (Chr(KeyNum) == Left(NoString, 1)) goto L015A;
    bConfigChanged = true;
    if (!(ProcessNo())) goto L015A;
    PlayModifySound();
    L015A:
    if (bExitAllMenus) goto L0169;
    ExitAllMenus();
    L0169:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Trigger.SpecialHandling

```unrealscript
function Actor SpecialHandling(Other: Pawn)
{
    if (!(bTriggerOnceOnly && !bCollideActors)) goto L0018;
    return None;
    L0018:
    if (!(TriggerType == 0 && !Other.bIsPlayer)) goto L003E;
    return None;
    L003E:
    if (bInitiallyActive) goto L00C8;
    if (TriggerActor != None) goto L005A;
    FindTriggerActor();
    L005A:
    if (TriggerActor != None) goto L0067;
    return None;
    L0067:
    if (!(TriggerActor2 != None && VSize(TriggerActor2.Location - Other.Location) < VSize(TriggerActor.Location - Other.Location))) goto L00C2;
    return TriggerActor2;
    goto L00C8;
    L00C2:
    return TriggerActor;
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerSwimming.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    GetAxes(ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    aForward *= 0.2;
    aStrafe *= 0.1;
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    aUp *= 0.1;
    newAccel = aForward * X + aStrafe * Y + aUp * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    if (bShowMenu) goto L0139;
    Speed2D = Sqrt(Velocity.X * Velocity.X + Velocity.Y * Velocity.Y);
    WalkBob = Y * Bob * 0.5 * Speed2D * Sin(4.0 * Level.TimeSeconds);
    WalkBob.Z = Bob * 1.5 * Speed2D * Sin(8.0 * Level.TimeSeconds);
    L0139:
    OldRotation = Rotation;
    UpdateRotation(DeltaTime, 2.0);
    if (Role >= 4) goto L0184;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, newAccel, 0, OldRotation - Rotation);
    goto L01A3;
    L0184:
    ProcessMove(DeltaTime, newAccel, 0, OldRotation - Rotation);
    L01A3:
    bPressedJump = false;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Inventory.CalcDrawOffset

```unrealscript
function Vector CalcDrawOffset()
{
    PawnOwner = class'Pawn'(Owner);
    DrawOffset = 0.9 / PawnOwner.FovAngle * PlayerViewOffset >> PawnOwner.ViewRotation;
    if (!(Level.NetMode == 1 || Level.NetMode == 2 && Owner.RemoteRole == 3)) goto L00B3;
    DrawOffset += PawnOwner.BaseEyeHeight * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    goto L0131;
    L00B3:
    DrawOffset += PawnOwner.EyeHeight * vect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    WeaponBob = BobDamping * PawnOwner.WalkBob;
    WeaponBob.Z = (0.45 + 0.55 * BobDamping) * PawnOwner.WalkBob.Z;
    DrawOffset += WeaponBob;
    L0131:
    return DrawOffset;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void TeamBroadcast(Msg: string)

    P.TeamMessage(PlayerReplicationInfo, Msg, 'Say');
    L0107:
    P = P.nextPawn;
    goto L00B6;
    L011E:
    return;
    L0120:
    if (!(Level.Game.AllowsBroadcast(self, Len(Msg)))) goto L01EB;
    P = Level.PawnList;
    L0157:

    L01D4:

    goto L0157;
    L01EB:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function float BotDesireability(Bot: Pawn)
{

    AlreadyHas = class'Weapon'(Bot.FindInventoryType(Class));
    if (AlreadyHas == None) goto L0186;
    if (!(RespawnTime < 10 && (bHidden || AlreadyHas.AmmoType == None || AlreadyHas.AmmoType.AmmoAmount < AlreadyHas.AmmoType.MaxAmmo))) goto L00B0;

    L00B0:

    return 0.0;
    L00D7:
    if (AlreadyHas.AmmoType != None) goto L00F8;
    return 0.25 * desire;
    L00F8:
    if (AlreadyHas.AmmoType.AmmoAmount <= 0) goto L0180;
    return FMax(0.25 * desire, AlreadyHas.AmmoType.MaxDesireability * FMin(1.0, 0.15 * AlreadyHas.AmmoType.MaxAmmo / AlreadyHas.AmmoType.AmmoAmount));
    goto L0186;
    L0180:
    return 0.05;
    L0186:
    if (!(Bot.Weapon == None || Bot.Weapon.AIRating <= 0.4)) goto L01CA;
    return 2.0 * desire;
    L01CA:
    return desire;
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
    L00F9:

}
```

## Engine.Weapon.ProjectileFire

```unrealscript
function Projectile ProjectileFire(ProjClass: class<Class>, projSpeed: float, bWarn: bool)
{
    PawnOwner = class'Pawn'(Owner);
    Owner.MakeNoise(PawnOwner.SoundDampening);
    GetAxes(PawnOwner.ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    Start = Owner.Location + CalcDrawOffset() + FireOffset.X * X + FireOffset.Y * Y + FireOffset.Z * Z;
    AdjustedAim = PawnOwner.AdjustAim(projSpeed, Start, aimerror, true, bWarn);
    return Spawn(ProjClass, None, None, Start, AdjustedAim);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Weapon.TraceFire

```unrealscript
function void TraceFire(Accuracy: float)
{
    PawnOwner = class'Pawn'(Owner);
    Owner.MakeNoise(PawnOwner.SoundDampening);
    GetAxes(PawnOwner.ViewRotation, X, Y, Z);
    StartTrace = Owner.Location + CalcDrawOffset() + FireOffset.X * X + FireOffset.Y * Y + FireOffset.Z * Z;
    AdjustedAim = PawnOwner.AdjustAim(1000000.0, StartTrace, 2 * aimerror, false, false);
    EndTrace = StartTrace + Accuracy * (FRand() - 0.5) * Y * 1000 + Accuracy * (FRand() - 0.5) * Z * 1000;
    X = AdjustedAim;
    EndTrace += 10000 * X;
    Other = PawnOwner.TraceShot(HitLocation, HitNormal, EndTrace, StartTrace);
    ProcessTraceHit(Other, HitLocation, HitNormal, X, Y, Z);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Weapon.Finish

```unrealscript
function void Finish()
{
    if (bChangeWeapon) goto L0012;
    GotoState('DownWeapon');
    return;
    L0012:
    PawnOwner = class'Pawn'(Owner);
    if (class'PlayerPawn'(Owner) != None) goto L00FE;
    if (!(AmmoType != None && AmmoType.AmmoAmount <= 0)) goto L0084;
    PawnOwner.StopFiring();
    PawnOwner.SwitchToBestWeapon();
    if (bChangeWeapon) goto L0081;
    GotoState('DownWeapon');
    L0081:
    goto L00FC;
    L0084:
    if (!(PawnOwner.bFire != 0 && FRand() < RefireRate)) goto L00B5;
    Fire(0.0);
    goto L00FC;
    L00B5:
    if (!(PawnOwner.bAltFire != 0 && FRand() < AltRefireRate)) goto L00E6;
    AltFire(0.0);
    goto L00FC;
    L00E6:
    PawnOwner.StopFiring();
    GotoState('Idle');
    L00FC:
    return;
    L00FE:
    if (!(AmmoType != None && AmmoType.AmmoAmount <= 0 || PawnOwner.Weapon != self)) goto L013F;
    GotoState('Idle');
    goto L018C;
    L013F:
    if (PawnOwner.bFire == 0) goto L0162;
    Fire(0.0);
    goto L018C;
    L0162:
    if (PawnOwner.bAltFire == 0) goto L0185;
    AltFire(0.0);
    goto L018C;
    L0185:
    GotoState('Idle');
    L018C:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void Frob(Other: Actor, frobWith: Inventory)

    Copy.Activate();
    L00F2:

    class'Pawn'(Other).ClientMessage(PickupMessage @ ItemArticle @ ItemName, 'Pickup');
    goto L0152;
    L012C:
    class'Pawn'(Other).ReceiveLocalizedMessage(PickupMessageClass, 0, None, None, self.Class);
    L0152:
    PlaySound(PickupSound, None, 2.0);
    class'Pickup'(Copy).PickupFunction(class'Pawn'(Other));
    L017E:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Ammo.BotDesireability

```unrealscript
function float BotDesireability(Bot: Pawn)
{
    if (ParentAmmo == None) goto L002D;
    AlreadyHas = class'Ammo'(Bot.FindInventoryType(ParentAmmo));
    goto L004C;
    L002D:
    AlreadyHas = class'Ammo'(Bot.FindInventoryType(Class));
    L004C:
    if (AlreadyHas != None) goto L0064;
    return 0.35 * MaxDesireability;
    L0064:
    if (AlreadyHas.AmmoAmount != 0) goto L007E;
    return MaxDesireability;
    L007E:
    if (AlreadyHas.AmmoAmount < AlreadyHas.MaxAmmo) goto L00A5;
    return -1.0;
    L00A5:
    return MaxDesireability * FMin(1.0, 0.15 * MaxAmmo / AlreadyHas.AmmoAmount);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Ammo.HandlePickupQuery

```unrealscript
function bool HandlePickupQuery(Item: Inventory)
{
    if (!(Class == Item.Class || ClassIsChildOf(Item.Class, Ammo) && Class == class'Ammo'(Item).ParentAmmo)) goto L01D2;
    if (AmmoAmount != MaxAmmo) goto L0063;
    return true;
    L0063:
    if (Level.Game.LocalLog == None) goto L00B0;
    Level.Game.LocalLog.LogPickup(Item, class'Pawn'(Owner));
    L00B0:
    if (Level.Game.WorldLog == None) goto L00FD;
    Level.Game.WorldLog.LogPickup(Item, class'Pawn'(Owner));
    L00FD:
    if (Item.PickupMessageClass != None) goto L015B;
    class'Pawn'(Owner).ClientMessage(Item.PickupMessage @ Item.ItemArticle @ Item.ItemName, 'Pickup');
    goto L018E;
    L015B:
    class'Pawn'(Owner).ReceiveLocalizedMessage(Item.PickupMessageClass, 0, None, None, Item.Class);
    L018E:
    Item.PlaySound(Item.PickupSound);
    AddAmmo(class'Ammo'(Item).AmmoAmount);
    Item.SetRespawn();
    return true;
    L01D2:
    if (Inventory != None) goto L01DF;
    return false;
    L01DF:
    return Inventory.HandlePickupQuery(Item);
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function Inventory SpawnCopy(Other: Pawn)
{
    if (ParentAmmo == None) goto L00E4;
    Copy = Spawn(ParentAmmo, Other, None, None, rot(0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    Copy.Tag = Tag;
    Copy.Event = Event;
    Copy.Instigator = Other;
    class'Ammo'(Copy).AmmoAmount = AmmoAmount;
    Copy.BecomeItem();
    Other.AddInventory(Copy);
    Copy.GotoState('None');
    if (!(Level.Game.ShouldRespawn(self))) goto L00DB;
    GotoState('Sleeping');
    goto L00DE;
    L00DB:
    Destroy();
    L00DE:
    return Copy;
    L00E4:
    Copy = Pickup.SpawnCopy(Other);
    class'Ammo'(Copy).AmmoAmount = AmmoAmount;
    return Copy;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.AddDefaultInventory

```unrealscript
function void AddDefaultInventory(PlayerPawn: Pawn)
{
    PlayerPawn.JumpZ = PlayerPawn.default.JumpZ * PlayerJumpZScaling();
    if (!(PlayerPawn.IsA('Spectator'))) goto L003B;
    return;
    L003B:
    WeapClass = BaseMutator.MutatedDefaultWeapon();
    if (!(WeapClass != None && PlayerPawn.FindInventoryType(WeapClass) == None)) goto L0112;
    NewWeapon = Spawn(WeapClass);
    if (NewWeapon == None) goto L0112;
    NewWeapon.Instigator = PlayerPawn;
    NewWeapon.BecomeItem();
    NewWeapon.GiveAmmo(PlayerPawn);
    PlayerPawn.AddInventory(NewWeapon);
    NewWeapon.BringUp();
    NewWeapon.SetSwitchPriority(PlayerPawn);
    NewWeapon.WeaponSet(PlayerPawn);
    L0112:
    BaseMutator.ModifyPlayer(PlayerPawn);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.FindPlayerStart

```unrealscript
function NavigationPoint FindPlayerStart(Player: Pawn, InTeam: byte?, incomingName: string?)
{
    if (incomingName == "") goto L003E;
    foreach (AllActors(Teleporter, Tel))  // end L003D
    if (Tel.Tag == incomingName) goto L003C;
    return Tel;
    L003C:
    continue;
    L003D:
    L003E:
    foreach (AllActors(PlayerStart, dest))  // end L007C
    if (!(dest.bSinglePlayerStart && dest.bEnabled)) goto L007B;
    return dest;
    L007B:
    continue;
    L007C:
    Log("WARNING: All single player starts were disabled - picking one anyway!");
    foreach (AllActors(PlayerStart, dest))  // end L00F0
    if (!(dest.bSinglePlayerStart)) goto L00EF;
    return dest;
    L00EF:
    continue;
    L00F0:
    Log("No single player start found");
    return None;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Fragment.CalcVelocity

```unrealscript
function void CalcVelocity(Momentum: Vector, ExplosionSize: float)
{
    Velocity = VRand() * (ExplosionSize + FRand() * 150.0 + 100.0 + VSize(Momentum) / 80);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.GameInfo.RestartPlayer

```unrealscript
function bool RestartPlayer(aPlayer: Pawn)
{
    if (!(bRestartLevel && Level.NetMode != 1 && Level.NetMode != 2)) goto L003D;
    return true;
    L003D:
    StartSpot = FindPlayerStart(aPlayer, 255);
    if (StartSpot != None) goto L007C;
    Log(" Player start not found!!!");
    return false;
    L007C:
    foundStart = aPlayer.SetLocation(StartSpot.Location);
    if (foundStart) goto L01DA;
    StartSpot.PlayTeleportEffect(aPlayer, true);
    aPlayer.SetRotation(StartSpot.Rotation);
    aPlayer.ViewRotation = aPlayer.Rotation;
    aPlayer.Acceleration = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    aPlayer.Velocity = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    aPlayer.Health = aPlayer.default.Health;
    aPlayer.SetCollision(true, true, true);
    aPlayer.ClientSetLocation(StartSpot.Location, StartSpot.Rotation);
    aPlayer.bHidden = false;
    aPlayer.DamageScaling = aPlayer.default.DamageScaling;
    aPlayer.SoundDampening = aPlayer.default.SoundDampening;
    AddDefaultInventory(aPlayer);
    goto L0202;
    L01DA:
    Log(StartSpot $ " Player start not useable!!!");
    L0202:
    return foundStart;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Fragment.Flying.ZoneChange

```unrealscript
function void ZoneChange(NewZone: ZoneInfo)
{
    if (!(NewZone.bWaterZone)) goto L0144;
    Velocity = 0.2 * Velocity;
    splashSize = 0.0005 * (250 - 0.5 * Velocity.Z);
    if (Level.NetMode == 1) goto L00DB;
    if (NewZone.EntrySound == None) goto L008A;
    PlaySound(NewZone.EntrySound, 3, splashSize);
    L008A:
    if (NewZone.EntryActor == None) goto L00DB;
    splash = Spawn(NewZone.EntryActor);
    if (splash == None) goto L00DB;
    splash.DrawScale = 4.0 * splashSize;
    L00DB:
    if (bFirstHit) goto L012A;
    bFirstHit = false;
    bRotateToDesired = true;
    bFixedRotationDir = false;
    DesiredRotation.Pitch = 0;
    DesiredRotation.Yaw = FRand() * 65536;
    DesiredRotation.Roll = 0;
    L012A:
    RotationRate = 0.2 * RotationRate;
    GotoState('Dying');
    L0144:
    if (!(NewZone.bPainZone && NewZone.DamagePerSec > 0)) goto L016F;
    Destroy();
    L016F:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Fragment.Dying.HitWall

```unrealscript
function void HitWall(HitNormal: Vector, HitWall: Actor)
{
    Velocity = elasticity * (Dot_VectorVector(Velocity, HitNormal) * HitNormal * -2.0 + Velocity);
    speed = VSize(Velocity);
    if (!(bFirstHit && speed < 400)) goto L009C;
    bFirstHit = false;
    bRotateToDesired = true;
    bFixedRotationDir = false;
    DesiredRotation.Pitch = 0;
    DesiredRotation.Yaw = FRand() * 65536;
    DesiredRotation.Roll = 0;
    L009C:
    RotationRate.Yaw = RotationRate.Yaw * 0.75;
    RotationRate.Roll = RotationRate.Roll * 0.75;
    RotationRate.Pitch = RotationRate.Pitch * 0.75;
    if (!(Velocity.Z < 50 && HitNormal.Z > 0.7)) goto L012E;
    SetPhysics(0);
    bBounce = false;
    goto L01AA;
    L012E:
    if (speed <= 80) goto L01AA;
    if (FRand() >= 0.5) goto L017A;
    PlaySound(ImpactSound, 0, 0.5 + FRand() * 0.5, None, 300.0, 0.85 + FRand() * 0.3);
    goto L01AA;
    L017A:
    PlaySound(MiscSound, 0, 0.5 + FRand() * 0.5, None, 300.0, 0.85 + FRand() * 0.3);
    L01AA:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.TriggerLight.Tick

```unrealscript
function void Tick(DeltaTime: float)
{
    Alpha += Direction * DeltaTime / ChangeTime;
    if (Alpha <= 1.0) goto L0058;
    Alpha = 1.0;
    Disable('Tick');
    if (SavedTrigger == None) goto L0055;
    SavedTrigger.EndEvent();
    L0055:
    goto L0093;
    L0058:
    if (Alpha >= 0.0) goto L0093;
    Alpha = 0.0;
    Disable('Tick');
    if (SavedTrigger == None) goto L0093;
    SavedTrigger.EndEvent();
    L0093:
    if (bDelayFullOn) goto L00B4;
    LightBrightness = Alpha * InitialBrightness;
    goto L00F3;
    L00B4:
    if (!(Direction > 0 && Alpha != 1 || Alpha == 0)) goto L00E7;
    LightBrightness = 0;
    goto L00F3;
    L00E7:
    LightBrightness = InitialBrightness;
    L00F3:
    if (LightBrightness != 0) goto L0116;
    MultiSkins[1] = Skin;
    Skin = None;
    goto L012C;
    L0116:
    Skin = MultiSkins[1];
    MultiSkins[1] = None;
    L012C:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
function void ScoreKill(Killer: Pawn, Other: Pawn)
{
    Other.DieCount++;
    if (!(Killer == Other || Killer == None)) goto L004A;
    SubtractEqual_FloatFloat(Other.PlayerReplicationInfo.Score, 1);
    goto L0094;
    L004A:
    if (Killer == None) goto L0094;
    Killer.KillCount++;
    if (Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo == None) goto L0094;
    AddEqual_FloatFloat(Killer.PlayerReplicationInfo.Score, 1);
    L0094:
    BaseMutator.ScoreKill(Killer, Other);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerWalking.PlayerMove

```unrealscript
function void PlayerMove(DeltaTime: float)
{
    GetAxes(Rotation, X, Y, Z);
    aForward *= 0.4;
    aStrafe *= 0.4;
    aLookUp *= 0.24;
    aTurn *= 0.24;
    newAccel = aForward * X + aStrafe * Y;
    newAccel.Z = 0.0;
    if (DodgeDir != 5) goto L008F;
    DodgeMove = 5;
    goto L0097;
    L008F:
    DodgeMove = 0;
    L0097:
    if (DodgeClickTime <= 0.0) goto L0213;
    if (DodgeDir >= 5) goto L018B;
    OldDodge = DodgeDir;
    DodgeDir = 0;
    if (!(bEdgeForward && bWasForward)) goto L00E3;
    DodgeDir = 3;
    L00E3:
    if (!(bEdgeBack && bWasBack)) goto L00FF;
    DodgeDir = 4;
    L00FF:
    if (!(bEdgeLeft && bWasLeft)) goto L011B;
    DodgeDir = 1;
    L011B:
    if (!(bEdgeRight && bWasRight)) goto L0137;
    DodgeDir = 2;
    L0137:
    if (DodgeDir != 0) goto L0153;
    DodgeDir = OldDodge;
    goto L018B;
    L0153:
    if (DodgeDir == OldDodge) goto L0180;
    DodgeClickTimer = DodgeClickTime + 0.5 * DeltaTime;
    goto L018B;
    L0180:
    DodgeMove = DodgeDir;
    L018B:
    if (DodgeDir != 6) goto L01CA;
    DodgeClickTimer -= DeltaTime;
    if (DodgeClickTimer >= -0.35) goto L01C7;
    DodgeDir = 0;
    DodgeClickTimer = DodgeClickTime;
    L01C7:
    goto L0213;
    L01CA:
    if (!(DodgeDir != 0 && DodgeDir != 5)) goto L0213;
    DodgeClickTimer -= DeltaTime;
    if (DodgeClickTimer >= 0) goto L0213;
    DodgeDir = 0;
    DodgeClickTimer = DodgeClickTime;
    L0213:
    AnimGroupName = GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence);
    if (!(Physics == 1 && AnimGroupName != 'Dodge')) goto L036D;
    if (!(!bKeyboardLook && bLook == 0)) goto L0314;
    if (bLookUpStairs) goto L0278;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = FindStairRotation(DeltaTime);
    goto L0314;
    L0278:
    if (bCenterView) goto L0314;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = ViewRotation.Pitch & 65535;
    if (ViewRotation.Pitch <= 32768) goto L02C2;
    ViewRotation.Pitch -= 65536;
    L02C2:
    ViewRotation.Pitch = ViewRotation.Pitch * (1 - 12 * FMin(0.0833, DeltaTime));
    if (Abs(ViewRotation.Pitch) >= 1000) goto L0314;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 0;
    L0314:
    Speed2D = Sqrt(Velocity.X * Velocity.X + Velocity.Y * Velocity.Y);
    if (bShowMenu) goto L036A;
    CheckBob(DeltaTime, Speed2D, Y);
    L036A:
    goto L03A7;
    L036D:
    if (bShowMenu) goto L03A7;
    bobtime = 0.0;
    WalkBob = WalkBob * (1 - FMin(1.0, 8.0 * DeltaTime));
    L03A7:
    OldRotation = Rotation;
    UpdateRotation(DeltaTime, 1.0);
    if (!(bPressedJump && AnimGroupName == 'Dodge')) goto L03EF;
    bSaveJump = true;
    bPressedJump = false;
    goto L03F7;
    L03EF:
    bSaveJump = false;
    L03F7:
    if (Role >= 4) goto L042A;
    ReplicateMove(DeltaTime, newAccel, DodgeMove, OldRotation - Rotation);
    goto L044C;
    L042A:
    ProcessMove(DeltaTime, newAccel, DodgeMove, OldRotation - Rotation);
    L044C:
    bPressedJump = bSaveJump;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerWalking.ProcessMove

```unrealscript
function void ProcessMove(DeltaTime: float, newAccel: Vector, DodgeMove: EDodgeDir, DeltaRot: Rotator)
{
    OldAccel = Acceleration;
    Acceleration = newAccel;
    bIsTurning = Abs(DeltaRot.Yaw / DeltaTime) > 5000;
    if (!(DodgeMove == 5 && Physics == 2)) goto L0062;
    DodgeDir = 5;
    goto L008B;
    L0062:
    if (!(DodgeMove != 0 && DodgeMove < 5)) goto L008B;
    Dodge(DodgeMove);
    L008B:
    if (bPressedJump) goto L009A;
    DoJump();
    L009A:
    if (!(Physics == 1 && GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) != 'Dodge')) goto L02D1;
    if (bIsCrouching) goto L00E4;
    if (bDuck == 0) goto L00E1;
    bIsCrouching = true;
    PlayDuck();
    L00E1:
    goto L0116;
    L00E4:
    if (bDuck != 0) goto L0116;
    OldAccel = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    bIsCrouching = false;
    TweenToRunning(0.1);
    L0116:
    if (bIsCrouching) goto L025D;
    if (!((!bAnimTransition || AnimFrame > 0) && GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) != 'Landing')) goto L025A;
    if (Acceleration == vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)) goto L01B5;
    if (!(GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) == 'Waiting' || GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) == 'Gesture' || GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) == 'TakeHit')) goto L01B2;
    bAnimTransition = true;
    TweenToRunning(0.1);
    L01B2:
    goto L025A;
    L01B5:
    if (!(Velocity.X * Velocity.X + Velocity.Y * Velocity.Y < 1000 && GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) != 'Gesture')) goto L025A;
    if (GetAnimGroup(AnimSequence) != 'Waiting') goto L023C;
    if (!(bIsTurning && AnimFrame >= 0)) goto L0239;
    bAnimTransition = true;
    PlayTurning();
    L0239:
    goto L025A;
    L023C:
    if (bIsTurning) goto L025A;
    bAnimTransition = true;
    TweenToWaiting(0.2);
    L025A:
    goto L02D1;
    L025D:
    if (!(OldAccel == vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0) && Acceleration != vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0))) goto L0296;
    PlayCrawling();
    goto L02D1;
    L0296:
    if (!(!bIsTurning && Acceleration == vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0) && AnimFrame > 0.1)) goto L02D1;
    PlayDuck();
    L02D1:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.Message

```unrealscript
function void Message(PRI: PlayerReplicationInfo, Msg: string, N: name)
{
    if (Msg == "") goto L0093;
    TopLine = (TopLine + 1) % 64;
    numLines = Min(numLines + 1, 64 - 1);
    MsgType[TopLine] = N;
    MsgTime = 6.0;
    TextLines++;
    MsgText[TopLine] = Msg;
    MsgPlayer[TopLine] = PRI;
    MsgTick[TopLine] = MsgTickTime + MsgTime;
    L0093:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.Tick

```unrealscript
function void Tick(Delta: float)
{
    MsgTickTime += Delta;
    if (ConsolePos >= ConsoleDest) goto L0037;
    ConsolePos = FMin(ConsolePos + Delta, ConsoleDest);
    goto L005F;
    L0037:
    if (ConsolePos <= ConsoleDest) goto L005F;
    ConsolePos = FMax(ConsolePos - Delta, ConsoleDest);
    L005F:
    if (!(MsgTime -= Delta <= 0.0 && TextLines > 0)) goto L0089;
    TextLines--;
    L0089:
    if (Viewport.Actor.ProgressTimeOut <= Viewport.Actor.Level.TimeSeconds) goto L00DD;
    Viewport.Actor.ShowProgress();
    L00DD:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.DrawLevelAction

```unrealscript
function void DrawLevelAction(C: Canvas)
{
    if (!(Viewport.Actor.bShowMenu)) goto L0025;
    BigMessage = "";
    return;
    L0025:
    if (!(Viewport.Actor.Level.Pauser != "" && Viewport.Actor.Level.LevelAction == 0)) goto L010A;
    C.Font = C.BigFont;
    C.Style = 1;
    C.DrawColor.R = 255;
    C.DrawColor.G = 255;
    C.DrawColor.B = 255;
    BigMessage = PausedMessage;
    PrintActionMessage(C, BigMessage);
    return;
    goto L01E3;
    L010A:
    if (Viewport.Actor.Level.LevelAction != 1) goto L0141;
    BigMessage = LoadingMessage;
    goto L01E3;
    L0141:
    if (Viewport.Actor.Level.LevelAction != 2) goto L0178;
    BigMessage = SavingMessage;
    goto L01E3;
    L0178:
    if (Viewport.Actor.Level.LevelAction != 3) goto L01AF;
    BigMessage = ConnectingMessage;
    goto L01E3;
    L01AF:
    if (Viewport.Actor.Level.LevelAction != 4) goto L01E3;
    BigMessage = PrecachingMessage;
    L01E3:
    if (BigMessage == "") goto L0270;
    C.Style = 3;
    C.DrawColor.R = 0;
    C.DrawColor.G = 0;
    C.DrawColor.B = 255;
    C.Font = C.LargeFont;
    PrintActionMessage(C, BigMessage);
    L0270:
    return;
}
```

```unrealscript
}
```

## Engine.Console.PostRender

```unrealscript
function void PostRender(C: Canvas)
{
    if (bNoDrawWorld) goto L0057;
    C.Canvas.SetPos(0.0, 0.0);
    C.Canvas.DrawPattern(Border, C.ClipX, C.ClipY, 1.0);
    L0057:
    if (bTimeDemo) goto L0071;
    TimeDemoCalc();
    TimeDemoRender(C);
    L0071:
    DrawLevelAction(C);
    if (ConsoleLines <= 0) goto L0143;
    C.Style = 1;
    C.DrawColor.R = 128;
    C.DrawColor.G = 128;
    C.DrawColor.B = 128;
    C.Canvas.SetPos(0.0, ConsoleLines - FrameY);
    C.Canvas.SetOrigin(0.0, 0.0);
    C.DrawTile(ConBackground, FrameX, FrameY, 0.0, 0.0, FrameX, FrameY);
    L0143:
    if (!(BorderLines > 0 || BorderPixels > 0)) goto L02D8;
    YStart = BorderLines + ConsoleLines;
    YEnd = FrameY - BorderLines;
    if (BorderLines <= 0) goto L0220;
    C.Canvas.SetOrigin(0.0, 0.0);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(0.0, 0.0);
    C.Canvas.DrawPattern(Border, FrameX, BorderLines, 1.0);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(0.0, YEnd);
    C.Canvas.DrawPattern(Border, FrameX, BorderLines, 1.0);
    L0220:
    if (BorderPixels <= 0) goto L02D8;
    C.Canvas.SetOrigin(0.0, 0.0);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(0.0, YStart);
    C.Canvas.DrawPattern(Border, BorderPixels, YEnd - YStart, 1.0);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(FrameX - BorderPixels, YStart);
    C.Canvas.DrawPattern(Border, BorderPixels, YEnd - YStart, 1.0);
    L02D8:
    C.Canvas.SetOrigin(0.0, 0.0);
    if (ConsoleLines <= 0) goto L030A;
    DrawConsoleView(C);
    goto L0315;
    L030A:
    DrawSingleView(C);
    L0315:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.DrawConsoleView

```unrealscript
function void DrawConsoleView(C: Canvas)

    i = Scrollback;
    L003E:

    C.Font = C.MedFont;
    if (!(MsgType[Line] == 'Say' || MsgType[Line] == 'TeamSay')) goto L0141;

    goto L0162;
    L0141:
    C.StrLen(MsgText[Line], XL, YL);

    Y -= YL;
    if (Y + YL >= 0) goto L019D;
    goto L0258;
    L019D:
    C.Canvas.SetPos(4.0, Y);
    C.Font = C.MedFont;
    if (!(MsgType[Line] == 'Say' || MsgType[Line] == 'TeamSay')) goto L0236;

    goto L024E;
    L0236:
    C.DrawText(MsgText[Line], false);
    L024E:
    i++;
    goto L003E;
    L0258:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.DrawSingleView

```unrealscript
function void DrawSingleView(C: Canvas)
{
    C.Canvas.SetOrigin(0.0, 0.0);
    if (!(Viewport.Actor.myHUD != None && Viewport.Actor.myHUD.DisplayMessages(C))) goto L0063;
    return;
    L0063:
    C.DrawColor.R = 255;
    C.DrawColor.G = 255;
    C.DrawColor.B = 255;

    if (bTyping) goto L015A;
    TypingPrompt = "(>" @ TypedStr $ "_";
    C.Font = C.MedFont;
    C.StrLen(TypingPrompt, XL, YL);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(2.0, FrameY - ConsoleLines - YL - 1);
    C.DrawText(TypingPrompt, false);
    L015A:
    if (!(TextLines > 0 && (!Viewport.Actor.bShowMenu || Viewport.Actor.bShowScores))) goto L0364;
    j = TopLine;
    i = 0;
    L01B3:
    if (!(i < 4 && j >= 0)) goto L027E;
    if (!(MsgText[j] != "" && MsgTick[j] > 0.0 && MsgTick[j] > MsgTickTime)) goto L0274;
    if (MsgType[j] != 'Say') goto L0256;
    ShortMessages[i] = MsgPlayer[j].PlayerName $ ":" @ MsgText[j];
    goto L026D;
    L0256:
    ShortMessages[i] = MsgText[j];
    L026D:
    i++;
    L0274:
    j--;
    goto L01B3;
    L027E:
    j = 0;
    C.Font = C.MedFont;
    i = 0;
    L02A9:
    if (i >= 4) goto L0364;
    if (ShortMessages[3 - i] == "") goto L035A;
    C.Canvas.SetPos(4.0, 2.0 + 10 * j + 10 * ExtraSpace);
    C.StrLen(ShortMessages[3 - i], XL, YL);
    C.DrawText(ShortMessages[3 - i], false);
    if (YL != 18.0) goto L0353;
    ExtraSpace++;
    L0353:
    j++;
    L035A:
    i++;
    goto L02A9;
    L0364:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.Typing.KeyType

```unrealscript
function bool KeyType(Key: EInputKey)
{
    if (Key != Asc("~")) goto L003E;
    if (ConsoleDest != 0.0) goto L0034;

    GotoState('Typing');
    goto L003B;
    L0034:
    GotoState('None');
    L003B:
    goto L00AF;
    L003E:
    if (!(Key >= 32 && Key < 128 && Key != Asc("~") && Key != Asc("`"))) goto L00AF;
    if (bNoStuff) goto L0091;
    bNoStuff = false;
    return true;
    L0091:
    TypedStr = TypedStr $ Chr(Key);
    Scrollback = 0;
    return true;
    L00AF:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.Typing.KeyEvent

```unrealscript
function bool KeyEvent(Key: EInputKey, Action: EInputAction, Delta: float)
{
    bNoStuff = false;
    if (Key != 27) goto L005E;
    if (Scrollback == 0) goto L002B;
    Scrollback = 0;
    goto L0054;
    L002B:
    if (TypedStr == "") goto L0042;
    TypedStr = "";
    goto L0054;
    L0042:
    ConsoleDest = 0.0;
    GotoState('None');
    L0054:
    Scrollback = 0;
    goto L0300;
    L005E:
    if (!(KeyEvent(Key, Action, Delta))) goto L007B;
    return true;
    goto L0300;
    L007B:
    if (Action == 1) goto L008E;
    return false;
    goto L0300;
    L008E:
    if (Key != 13) goto L01AB;
    if (Scrollback == 0) goto L00B1;
    Scrollback = 0;
    goto L01A8;
    L00B1:
    if (TypedStr == "") goto L018B;
    if (ConsoleLines == 0) goto L00DF;
    Message(None, "(>" @ TypedStr, 'Console');
    L00DF:
    History[HistoryCur++ % 16] = TypedStr;
    if (HistoryCur <= HistoryBot) goto L010F;
    HistoryBot++;
    L010F:
    if (HistoryCur - HistoryTop < 16) goto L0134;
    HistoryTop = HistoryCur - 16 + 1;
    L0134:
    Temp = TypedStr;
    TypedStr = "";
    if (ConsoleCommand(Temp)) goto L017D;
    Message(None, Localize("Errors", "Exec", "Core"), 'Console');
    L017D:
    Message(None, "", 'Console');
    L018B:
    if (ConsoleDest != 0.0) goto L01A1;
    GotoState('None');
    L01A1:
    Scrollback = 0;
    L01A8:
    goto L0300;
    L01AB:
    if (Key != 38) goto L0204;
    if (HistoryCur <= HistoryTop) goto L01FA;
    History[HistoryCur % 16] = TypedStr;
    TypedStr = History[--HistoryCur % 16];
    L01FA:
    Scrollback = 0;
    goto L0300;
    L0204:
    if (Key != 40) goto L0268;
    History[HistoryCur % 16] = TypedStr;
    if (HistoryCur >= HistoryBot) goto L0256;
    TypedStr = History[++HistoryCur % 16];
    goto L025E;
    L0256:
    TypedStr = "";
    L025E:
    Scrollback = 0;
    goto L0300;
    L0268:
    if (Key != 33) goto L0292;
    if (++Scrollback < 64) goto L028F;
    Scrollback = 64 - 1;
    L028F:
    goto L0300;
    L0292:
    if (Key != 34) goto L02B7;
    if (--Scrollback >= 0) goto L02B4;
    Scrollback = 0;
    L02B4:
    goto L0300;
    L02B7:
    if (!(Key == 8 || Key == 37)) goto L0300;
    if (Len(TypedStr) <= 0) goto L02F9;
    TypedStr = Left(TypedStr, Len(TypedStr) - 1);
    L02F9:
    Scrollback = 0;
    L0300:
    return true;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.PrintTimeDemoResult

```unrealscript
function void PrintTimeDemoResult()
{
    Entry = Viewport.Actor.PlayerPawn.GetEntryLevel();
    Delta = Entry.TimeSeconds - StartTime - ExtraTime;
    if (Delta > 0) goto L005A;
    Avg = 0.0;
    goto L006D;
    L005A:
    Avg = FrameCount / Delta;
    L006D:
    AvgString = FrameCount @ FramesText @ Console.FormatFloat(Delta) @ SecondsText @ MinText @ Console.FormatFloat(MinFPS) @ MaxText @ Console.FormatFloat(MaxFPS) @ AvgText @ Console.FormatFloat(Avg) @ fpsText $ ".";
    Viewport.Actor.ClientMessage(AvgString);
    Log(AvgString);
    if (bSaveTimeDemoToFile) goto L01A1;
    Temp = Console.FormatFloat(Avg) $ " Unreal " $ Viewport.Actor.Level.EngineVersion $ Chr(13) $ Chr(10) $ Console.FormatFloat(MinFPS) $ " Min" $ Chr(13) $ Chr(10) $ Console.FormatFloat(MaxFPS) $ " Max" $ Chr(13) $ Chr(10);
    SaveTimeDemo(Temp);
    L01A1:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.TimeDemoCalc

```unrealscript
function void TimeDemoCalc()
{
    Entry = Viewport.Actor.PlayerPawn.GetEntryLevel();
    if (bRestartTimeDemo) goto L003B;
    StopTimeDemo();
    StartTimeDemo();
    bRestartTimeDemo = false;
    L003B:
    if (bStartTimeDemo) goto L00B2;
    bStartTimeDemo = false;
    StartTime = Entry.TimeSeconds;
    ExtraTime = 0.0;
    LastFrameTime = StartTime;
    LastSecondStartTime = StartTime;
    FrameCount = 0;
    LastSecondFrameCount = 0;
    MinFPS = 0.0;
    MaxFPS = 0.0;
    LastSecFPS = 0.0;
    return;
    L00B2:
    Delta = Entry.TimeSeconds - LastFrameTime;
    if (Delta <= 0.5) goto L0119;
    ExtraTime += Delta;
    LastSecondStartTime = Entry.TimeSeconds;
    LastSecondFrameCount = 0;
    LastFrameTime = Entry.TimeSeconds;
    return;
    L0119:
    FrameCount++;
    LastSecondFrameCount++;
    if (Entry.TimeSeconds - LastSecondStartTime <= 1) goto L01C3;
    LastSecFPS = LastSecondFrameCount / (Entry.TimeSeconds - LastSecondStartTime);
    if (!(MinFPS == 0 || LastSecFPS < MinFPS)) goto L018E;
    MinFPS = LastSecFPS;
    L018E:
    if (LastSecFPS <= MaxFPS) goto L01A8;
    MaxFPS = LastSecFPS;
    L01A8:
    LastSecondFrameCount = 0;
    LastSecondStartTime = Entry.TimeSeconds;
    L01C3:
    LastFrameTime = Entry.TimeSeconds;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Console.TimeDemoRender

```unrealscript
function void TimeDemoRender(C: Canvas)
{
    C.Font = TimeDemoFont;
    C.DrawColor.R = 255;
    C.DrawColor.G = 255;
    C.DrawColor.B = 255;
    AText = AvgText @ Console.FormatFloat(FrameCount / (Viewport.Actor.PlayerPawn.GetEntryLevel().TimeSeconds - StartTime - ExtraTime));
    LText = LastSecText @ Console.FormatFloat(LastSecFPS);
    C.TextSize(AText, W, H);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(C.ClipX - W, 0.3 * C.ClipY);
    C.DrawText(AText);
    C.TextSize(LText, W, H);
    C.Canvas.SetPos(C.ClipX - W, 0.3 * C.ClipY + H);
    C.DrawText(LText);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.ViewShake

```unrealscript
function void ViewShake(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (shaketimer <= 0.0) goto L0279;
    shaketimer -= DeltaTime;
    if (verttimer != 0) goto L0047;
    verttimer = 0.1;
    shakevert = -1.1 * maxshake;
    goto L0088;
    L0047:
    verttimer -= DeltaTime;
    if (verttimer >= 0) goto L0088;
    verttimer = 0.2 * FRand();
    shakevert = (2.0 * FRand() - 1) * maxshake;
    L0088:
    ViewRotation.Roll = ViewRotation.Roll & 65535;
    if (bShakeDir) goto L0187;
    ViewRotation.Roll += 10 * shakemag * FMin(0.1, DeltaTime);
    bShakeDir = ViewRotation.Roll > 32768 || ViewRotation.Roll < (0.5 + FRand()) * shakemag;
    if (!(ViewRotation.Roll < 32768 && ViewRotation.Roll > 1.3 * shakemag)) goto L0164;
    ViewRotation.Roll = 1.3 * shakemag;
    bShakeDir = false;
    goto L0184;
    L0164:
    if (FRand() >= 3 * DeltaTime) goto L0184;
    bShakeDir = !bShakeDir;
    L0184:
    goto L0276;
    L0187:
    ViewRotation.Roll -= 10 * shakemag * FMin(0.1, DeltaTime);
    bShakeDir = ViewRotation.Roll > 32768 && ViewRotation.Roll < 65535 - (0.5 + FRand()) * shakemag;
    if (!(ViewRotation.Roll > 32768 && ViewRotation.Roll < 65535 - 1.3 * shakemag)) goto L0256;
    ViewRotation.Roll = 65535 - 1.3 * shakemag;
    bShakeDir = true;
    goto L0276;
    L0256:
    if (FRand() >= 3 * DeltaTime) goto L0276;
    bShakeDir = !bShakeDir;
    L0276:
    goto L035F;
    L0279:
    shakevert = 0.0;
    ViewRotation.Roll = ViewRotation.Roll & 65535;
    if (ViewRotation.Roll >= 32768) goto L0307;
    if (ViewRotation.Roll <= 0) goto L0304;
    ViewRotation.Roll = Max(0, ViewRotation.Roll - Max(ViewRotation.Roll, 500) * 10 * FMin(0.1, DeltaTime));
    L0304:
    goto L035F;
    L0307:
    ViewRotation.Roll += (65536 - Max(500, ViewRotation.Roll)) * 10 * FMin(0.1, DeltaTime);
    if (ViewRotation.Roll <= 65534) goto L035F;
    ViewRotation.Roll = 0;
    L035F:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.UpdateRotation

```unrealscript
function void UpdateRotation(DeltaTime: float, maxPitch: float)
{
    DesiredRotation = ViewRotation;
    ViewRotation.Pitch += 32.0 * DeltaTime * aLookUp;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = ViewRotation.Pitch & 65535;
    if (!(ViewRotation.Pitch > 18000 && ViewRotation.Pitch < 49152)) goto L00A0;
    if (aLookUp <= 0) goto L0090;
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 18000;
    goto L00A0;
    L0090:
    ViewRotation.Pitch = 49152;
    L00A0:
    ViewRotation.Yaw += 32.0 * DeltaTime * aTurn;
    ViewShake(DeltaTime);
    ViewFlash(DeltaTime);
    NewRotation = Rotation;
    NewRotation.Yaw = ViewRotation.Yaw;
    NewRotation.Pitch = ViewRotation.Pitch;
    if (!(NewRotation.Pitch > maxPitch * RotationRate.Pitch && NewRotation.Pitch < 65536 - maxPitch * RotationRate.Pitch)) goto L01B4;
    if (ViewRotation.Pitch >= 32768) goto L018E;
    NewRotation.Pitch = maxPitch * RotationRate.Pitch;
    goto L01B4;
    L018E:
    NewRotation.Pitch = 65536 - maxPitch * RotationRate.Pitch;
    L01B4:
    NewRotation.Pitch = 0;
    NewRotation.Roll = 0;
    SetRotation(NewRotation);
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.ViewFlash

```unrealscript
function void ViewFlash(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (bNoFlash) goto L0027;
    InstantFlash = 0.0;
    InstantFog = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    L0027:
    Delta = FMin(0.1, DeltaTime);
    goalscale = 1.0 + DesiredFlashScale + ConstantGlowScale + HeadRegion.Zone.ViewFlash.X;
    goalFog = DesiredFlashFog + ConstantGlowFog + HeadRegion.Zone.ViewFog;
    DesiredFlashScale -= DesiredFlashScale * 2 * Delta;
    DesiredFlashFog -= DesiredFlashFog * 2 * Delta;
    FlashScale.X += (goalscale - FlashScale.X + InstantFlash) * 10 * Delta;
    FlashFog += (goalFog - FlashFog + InstantFog) * 10 * Delta;
    InstantFlash = 0.0;
    InstantFog = vect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    if (FlashScale.X <= 0.981) goto L015B;
    FlashScale.X = 1.0;
    L015B:
    FlashScale = FlashScale.X * vect(1.0, 1.0, 1.0);
    if (FlashFog.X >= 0.019) goto L019E;
    FlashFog.X = 0.0;
    L019E:
    if (FlashFog.Y >= 0.019) goto L01C2;
    FlashFog.Y = 0.0;
    L01C2:
    if (FlashFog.Z >= 0.019) goto L01E6;
    FlashFog.Z = 0.0;
    L01E6:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.PlayerInput

```unrealscript
function void PlayerInput(DeltaTime: float)
{
    if (!(bShowMenu && myHUD != None)) goto L00B8;
    if (myHUD.MainMenu == None) goto L0047;
    myHUD.MainMenu.MenuTick(DeltaTime);
    L0047:
    bEdgeForward = false;
    bEdgeBack = false;
    bEdgeLeft = false;
    bEdgeRight = false;
    bWasForward = false;
    bWasBack = false;
    bWasLeft = false;
    bWasRight = false;
    aStrafe = 0.0;
    aTurn = 0.0;
    aForward = 0.0;
    aLookUp = 0.0;
    return;
    goto L00D5;
    L00B8:
    if (bDelayedCommand) goto L00D5;
    bDelayedCommand = false;
    eatstring(ConsoleCommand(DelayedCommand));
    L00D5:
    bEdgeForward = bWasForward ^^ aBaseY > 0;
    bEdgeBack = bWasBack ^^ aBaseY < 0;
    bEdgeLeft = bWasLeft ^^ aStrafe > 0;
    bEdgeRight = bWasRight ^^ aStrafe < 0;
    bWasForward = aBaseY > 0;
    bWasBack = aBaseY < 0;
    bWasLeft = aStrafe > 0;
    bWasRight = aStrafe < 0;
    SmoothTime = FMin(0.2, 3.0 * DeltaTime * Level.TimeDilation);
    FOVScale = DesiredFOV * 0.01111;
    MouseScale = MouseSensitivity * FOVScale;
    aMouseX *= MouseScale;
    aMouseY *= MouseScale;
    AbsSmoothX = SmoothMouseX;
    AbsSmoothY = SmoothMouseY;
    MouseTime = (Level.TimeSeconds - MouseZeroTime) / Level.TimeDilation;
    if (!(bMaxMouseSmoothing && aMouseX == 0 && MouseTime < MouseSmoothThreshold)) goto L0279;
    SmoothMouseX = 0.5 * (MouseSmoothThreshold - MouseTime) * AbsSmoothX / MouseSmoothThreshold;
    BorrowedMouseX += SmoothMouseX;
    goto L033E;
    L0279:
    if (!(SmoothMouseX == 0 || aMouseX == 0 || SmoothMouseX > 0 != aMouseX > 0)) goto L02C5;
    SmoothMouseX = aMouseX;
    BorrowedMouseX = 0.0;
    goto L0333;
    L02C5:
    SmoothMouseX = 0.5 * (SmoothMouseX + aMouseX - BorrowedMouseX);
    if (SmoothMouseX > 0 == aMouseX > 0) goto L0321;
    if (aMouseX <= 0) goto L0316;
    SmoothMouseX = 1.0;
    goto L0321;
    L0316:
    SmoothMouseX = -1.0;
    L0321:
    BorrowedMouseX = SmoothMouseX - aMouseX;
    L0333:
    AbsSmoothX = SmoothMouseX;
    L033E:
    if (!(bMaxMouseSmoothing && aMouseY == 0 && MouseTime < MouseSmoothThreshold)) goto L039C;
    SmoothMouseY = 0.5 * (MouseSmoothThreshold - MouseTime) * AbsSmoothY / MouseSmoothThreshold;
    BorrowedMouseY += SmoothMouseY;
    goto L0461;
    L039C:
    if (!(SmoothMouseY == 0 || aMouseY == 0 || SmoothMouseY > 0 != aMouseY > 0)) goto L03E8;
    SmoothMouseY = aMouseY;
    BorrowedMouseY = 0.0;
    goto L0456;
    L03E8:
    SmoothMouseY = 0.5 * (SmoothMouseY + aMouseY - BorrowedMouseY);
    if (SmoothMouseY > 0 == aMouseY > 0) goto L0444;
    if (aMouseY <= 0) goto L0439;
    SmoothMouseY = 1.0;
    goto L0444;
    L0439:
    SmoothMouseY = -1.0;
    L0444:
    BorrowedMouseY = SmoothMouseY - aMouseY;
    L0456:
    AbsSmoothY = SmoothMouseY;
    L0461:
    if (!(aMouseX != 0 || aMouseY != 0)) goto L048F;
    MouseZeroTime = Level.TimeSeconds;
    L048F:
    aLookUp *= FOVScale;
    aTurn *= FOVScale;
    if (bStrafe == 0) goto L04D4;
    aStrafe += aBaseX + SmoothMouseX;
    aBaseX = 0.0;
    goto L051C;
    L04D4:
    aTurn += aBaseX * FOVScale + SmoothMouseX;
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0511;
    aTurn *= TurnRateAdjuster;
    L0511:
    aBaseX = 0.0;
    L051C:
    if (!(bStrafe == 0 && (bAlwaysMouseLook || bLook != 0))) goto L058B;
    if (bInvertMouse) goto L0559;
    aLookUp -= SmoothMouseY;
    goto L0565;
    L0559:
    aLookUp += SmoothMouseY;
    L0565:
    if (Level.NetMode == 0) goto L0588;
    aLookUp *= TurnRateAdjuster;
    L0588:
    goto L0597;
    L058B:
    aForward += SmoothMouseY;
    L0597:
    SmoothMouseX = AbsSmoothX;
    SmoothMouseY = AbsSmoothY;
    if (bSnapLevel == 0) goto L05CC;
    bCenterView = true;
    bKeyboardLook = false;
    goto L0611;
    L05CC:
    if (aLookUp == 0) goto L05EB;
    bCenterView = false;
    bKeyboardLook = true;
    goto L0611;
    L05EB:
    if (!(bSnapToLevel && !bAlwaysMouseLook)) goto L0611;
    bCenterView = true;
    bKeyboardLook = false;
    L0611:
    if (bFreeLook == 0) goto L0642;
    bKeyboardLook = true;
    aLookUp += 0.5 * aBaseY * FOVScale;
    goto L064E;
    L0642:
    aForward += aBaseY;
    L064E:
    aBaseY = 0.0;
    HandleWalking();
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.CalcBehindView

```unrealscript
function void CalcBehindView(out CameraLocation: Vector, out CameraRotation: Rotator, dist: float)
{
    CameraRotation = ViewRotation;
    View = vect(1.0, 0.0, 0.0) >> CameraRotation;
    if (Trace(HitLocation, HitNormal, CameraLocation - (dist + 30) * CameraRotation, CameraLocation) == None) goto L007A;
    ViewDist = FMin(Dot_VectorVector(CameraLocation - HitLocation, View), dist);
    goto L0085;
    L007A:
    ViewDist = dist;
    L0085:
    CameraLocation -= (ViewDist - 30) * View;
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.SpawnGibbedCarcass

```unrealscript
function void SpawnGibbedCarcass()
{
    carc = Spawn(CarcassType);
    if (carc == None) goto L0044;
    carc.Initfor(self);
    carc.ChunkUp(-1 * Health);
    L0044:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.PlayerPawn.ServerUpdateWeapons

```unrealscript
function void ServerUpdateWeapons()
{
    Inv = Inventory;
    L000B:
    if (Inv == None) goto L0056;
    if (!(Inv.IsA('Weapon'))) goto L003F;
    class'Weapon'(Inv).SetSwitchPriority(self);
    L003F:
    Inv = Inv.Inventory;
    goto L000B;
    L0056:
    return;
}
```

## Engine.Canvas.Reset

```unrealscript
function void Reset()
{
    Font = default.Font;
    SpaceX = default.SpaceX;
    SpaceY = default.SpaceY;
    OrgX = default.OrgX;
    OrgY = default.OrgY;
    CurX = default.CurX;
    CurY = default.CurY;
    Style = default.Style;
    DrawColor = default.DrawColor;
    CurYL = default.CurYL;
    bCenter = false;
    bNoSmooth = false;
    Z = 1.0;
    return;
}
```
